     1→import asyncio
     2→import logging
     3→from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
     4→from pathlib import Path
     5→
     6→from fastapi import FastAPI
     7→from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
     8→from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles
     9→from fastapi.responses import FileResponse
    10→
    11→from app.routes import tables, query, chat, langfuse_status, config, session
    12→from app.container_manager import container_manager
    13→from app.mcp_sse import mcp_app
    14→from app.proxy import router as proxy_router
    15→from app.config import CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS
    16→
    17→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    18→
    19→from app.session_manager import session_manager
    20→
    21→
    22→async def _cleanup_loop():
    23→    while True:
    24→        await asyncio.sleep(60)
    25→        try:
    26→            removed = session_manager.cleanup_stale(ttl_seconds=300)
    27→            if removed:
    28→                logger.info("Background cleanup: removed %d stale sessions", removed)
    29→            if container_manager is not None:
    30→                container_removed = container_manager.cleanup_expired()
    31→                if container_removed:
    32→                    logger.info("Background cleanup: removed %d expired containers", container_removed)
    33→        except Exception:
    34→            logger.exception("Error in background cleanup loop")
    35→
    36→
    37→@asynccontextmanager
    38→async def lifespan(app):
    39→    # Clean up orphaned sidecar containers from a previous unclean shutdown.
    40→    if container_manager is not None:
    41→        orphans = container_manager._cleanup_by_label()
    42→        if orphans:
    43→            logger.info("Startup: cleaned up %d orphaned sidecar containers", orphans)
    44→    task = asyncio.create_task(_cleanup_loop())
    45→    yield
    46→    task.cancel()
    47→    if container_manager is not None:
    48→        container_manager.shutdown_all()
    49→
    50→
    51→app = FastAPI(title="DuckDB Data Agent API", lifespan=lifespan)
    52→
    53→app.add_middleware(
    54→    CORSMiddleware,
    55→    allow_origins=CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS,
    56→    allow_credentials=True,
    57→    allow_methods=["*"],
    58→    allow_headers=["*"],
    59→)
    60→
    61→app.include_router(tables.router)
    62→app.include_router(query.router)
    63→app.include_router(chat.router)
    64→app.include_router(langfuse_status.router)
    65→app.include_router(config.router)
    66→app.include_router(session.router)
    67→app.include_router(proxy_router)
    68→
    69→
    70→app.mount("/mcp", mcp_app)
    71→
    72→
    73→@app.get("/api/health")
    74→async def health():
    75→    return {"status": "ok"}
    76→
    77→
    78→# Serve frontend static files in production
    79→STATIC_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "static"
    80→
    81→if STATIC_DIR.is_dir():
    82→    app.mount("/assets", StaticFiles(directory=STATIC_DIR / "assets"), name="static-assets")
    83→
    84→    @app.get("/{full_path:path}")
    85→    async def serve_frontend(full_path: str):
    86→        """Serve frontend for any non-API route (SPA fallback)."""
    87→        file_path = (STATIC_DIR / full_path).resolve()
    88→        # Prevent path traversal: ensure resolved path is inside STATIC_DIR
    89→        if full_path and file_path.is_file() and file_path.is_relative_to(STATIC_DIR.resolve()):
    90→            return FileResponse(file_path)
    91→        return FileResponse(STATIC_DIR / "index.html")
    92→

     1→import express, { Request, Response } from "express";
     2→import { query, AgentDefinition, SettingSource, HookCallbackMatcher } from "@anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk";
     3→import { Langfuse } from "langfuse";
     4→import { mkdirSync, writeFileSync, existsSync, readdirSync } from "fs";
     5→import { homedir } from "os";
     6→import { join } from "path";
     7→import type { QueryRequest, HealthResponse, AgentDefinitionPayload } from "./types.js";
     8→
     9→// Claude CLI requires certain directories/files under ~/.claude to exist.
    10→// The container uses a tmpfs mount at ~/.claude which starts empty, so we
    11→// create the expected structure at startup.
    12→const claudeDir = join(homedir(), ".claude");
    13→for (const sub of ["debug", "projects"]) {
    14→  const dir = join(claudeDir, sub);
    15→  if (!existsSync(dir)) {
    16→    mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
    17→  }
    18→}
    19→const settingsFile = join(claudeDir, "remote-settings.json");
    20→if (!existsSync(settingsFile)) {
    21→  writeFileSync(settingsFile, "{}");
    22→}
    23→
    24→// Discover allowed skills from .claude/skills/ directory at startup.
    25→// Only these skills may be invoked — built-in skills bundled with the
    26→// Claude Code binary (e.g. "simplify") are blocked.
    27→const skillsDir = join(process.cwd(), ".claude", "skills");
    28→const ALLOWED_SKILLS = new Set(
    29→  existsSync(skillsDir)
    30→    ? readdirSync(skillsDir, { withFileTypes: true })
    31→        .filter((d) => d.isDirectory() && existsSync(join(skillsDir, d.name, "SKILL.md")))
    32→        .map((d) => d.name)
    33→    : []
    34→);
    35→console.log(`[sidecar] Allowed skills: ${[...ALLOWED_SKILLS].join(", ") || "(none)"}`);
    36→
    37→const skillAllowlistHook: HookCallbackMatcher = {
    38→  matcher: "Skill",
    39→  hooks: [
    40→    async (input) => {
    41→      const toolInput = (input as Record<string, unknown>).tool_input as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
    42→      const skillName = toolInput?.skill as string | undefined;
    43→      if (skillName && !ALLOWED_SKILLS.has(skillName)) {
    44→        return {
    45→          hookSpecificOutput: {
    46→            hookEventName: "PreToolUse" as const,
    47→            permissionDecision: "deny" as const,
    48→            permissionDecisionReason: `Skill "${skillName}" is not in the allowlist.`,
    49→          },
    50→        };
    51→      }
    52→      return {};
    53→    },
    54→  ],
    55→};
    56→
    57→// Initialize Langfuse if credentials are available (reads from env vars
    58→// LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY, LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY, LANGFUSE_BASE_URL automatically)
    59→let langfuse: Langfuse | null = null;
    60→if (process.env.LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY && process.env.LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY) {
    61→  langfuse = new Langfuse();
    62→  console.log("[sidecar] Langfuse tracing enabled");
    63→}
    64→
    65→const app = express();
    66→app.use(express.json({ limit: "10mb" }));
    67→
    68→const PORT = parseInt(process.env.PORT || "3000", 10);
    69→
    70→// Per-request abort tracking — avoids a global variable whose cleanup in one
    71→// request's `finally` block can clobber a concurrently-started request.
    72→let nextRequestId = 0;
    73→const activeAborts = new Map<number, AbortController>();
    74→
    75→// How long (ms) to wait for the SDK iterator to yield a message before
    76→// aborting.  Resets on every message so active multi-turn queries are not
    77→// interrupted.  Covers cases where the CLI subprocess hangs on startup
    78→// (e.g. broken session resume, unreachable MCP server).
    79→const SDK_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS = parseInt(process.env.SDK_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS || "120000", 10);
    80→
    81→// Timeout (ms) for pre-flight reachability checks against MCP / API URLs.
    82→const PREFLIGHT_TIMEOUT_MS = 10_000; // 10 seconds
    83→
    84→app.get("/health", (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
    85→  const response: HealthResponse = { status: "ok" };
    86→  res.json(response);
    87→});
    88→
    89→app.post("/query", async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    90→  const body = req.body as QueryRequest;
    91→
    92→  if (!body.message || !body.system_prompt) {
    93→    res.status(400).json({ error: "message and system_prompt are required" });
    94→    return;
    95→  }
    96→
    97→  // Set up SSE headers
    98→  res.setHeader("Content-Type", "text/event-stream");
    99→  res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-cache");
   100→  res.setHeader("Connection", "keep-alive");
   101→  res.setHeader("X-Accel-Buffering", "no");
   102→  res.flushHeaders();
   103→
   104→  const abortController = new AbortController();
   105→  const requestId = nextRequestId++;
   106→  activeAborts.set(requestId, abortController);
   107→
   108→  // Merge per-request env overrides (e.g. Bifrost gateway URLs) with process env
   109→  const sdkEnv: Record<string, string> = {};
   110→  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(process.env)) {
   111→    if (v !== undefined) sdkEnv[k] = v;
   112→  }
   113→  if (body.env) {
   114→    Object.assign(sdkEnv, body.env);
   115→  }
   116→  // Scrub Langfuse credentials so the agent subprocess cannot read them from
   117→  // the inherited environment — matches backend subprocess behaviour.
   118→  // The sidecar's own Langfuse client already holds the credentials internally.
   119→  sdkEnv["LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY"] = "";
   120→  sdkEnv["LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY"] = "";
   121→
   122→  let responseEnded = false;
   123→
   124→  // Detect client disconnect
   125→  res.on("close", () => {
   126→    if (!responseEnded) {
   127→      abortController.abort();
   128→    }
   129→  });
   130→
   131→  // --- Langfuse tracing setup ---
   132→  // Mirror the backend's session ID handling:
   133→  //   - langfuse_session_id (from frontend for edit/delete) takes priority
   134→  //   - session_id (CLI session from previous turn) as fallback
   135→  const modelName = body.model || process.env.ORCHESTRATOR_MODEL || "claude-sonnet-4-6";
   136→  const traceMessage = body.original_message || body.message;
   137→  const traceInput: Record<string, unknown> = {
   138→    message: traceMessage.substring(0, 500),
   139→  };
   140→  if (body.conversation_history && body.conversation_history.length > 0) {
   141→    traceInput.conversation_history = body.conversation_history;
   142→  }
   143→  const trace = langfuse?.trace({
   144→    name: "agent-chat",
   145→    sessionId: body.langfuse_session_id || body.session_id || undefined,
   146→    input: traceInput,
   147→    metadata: { model: modelName, mode: "container" },
   148→  });
   149→
   150→  // Per-turn usage tracking from stream events
   151→  let currentGenUsage: { input?: number; output?: number } = {};
   152→  // Accumulated messages for generation input context
   153→  const accumulatedMessages: Array<{ role: string; content: unknown }> = [
   154→    { role: "user", content: body.message },
   155→  ];
   156→
   157→  // Collect stderr from the CLI subprocess for debugging
   158→  const stderrLines: string[] = [];
   159→
   160→  // Declared here so `finally` can clear it even if `try` throws early
   161→  let idleTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
   162→
   163→  try {
   164→    // --- Pre-flight reachability checks ---
   165→    // The CLI subprocess will hang silently if it can't reach the MCP SSE
   166→    // server or the Bifrost LLM gateway.  Test connectivity first so we can
   167→    // fail fast with a useful error message.
   168→    const apiBase = sdkEnv["ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL"] || "";
   169→    console.log(
   170→      `[sidecar] reqId=${requestId} mcp_url=${body.mcp_server_url || "(none)"} api_base=${apiBase} session_id=${body.session_id || "(none)"}`
   171→    );
   172→
   173→    if (body.mcp_server_url) {
   174→      try {
   175→        const mcpResp = await fetch(body.mcp_server_url, {
   176→          signal: AbortSignal.timeout(PREFLIGHT_TIMEOUT_MS),
   177→        });
   178→        // SSE endpoints normally return 200 with text/event-stream; any
   179→        // non-error status is fine — we just need to know the host is up.
   180→        mcpResp.body?.cancel(); // don't consume the stream
   181→        console.log(`[sidecar] MCP reachability OK (status=${mcpResp.status})`);
   182→      } catch (e: unknown) {
   183→        const reason = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);
   184→        throw new Error(
   185→          `MCP server unreachable at ${body.mcp_server_url}: ${reason}. ` +
   186→          `Check that BACKEND_BASE_URL is reachable from inside the container.`
   187→        );
   188→      }
   189→    }
   190→
   191→    if (apiBase) {
   192→      try {
   193→        // Just a quick TCP-level check — Bifrost will return 4xx without
   194→        // a real API key but that still proves reachability.
   195→        const apiResp = await fetch(`${apiBase}/v1/models`, {
   196→          signal: AbortSignal.timeout(PREFLIGHT_TIMEOUT_MS),
   197→        });
   198→        apiResp.body?.cancel();
   199→        console.log(`[sidecar] Bifrost LLM gateway reachability OK (status=${apiResp.status})`);
   200→      } catch (e: unknown) {
   201→        const reason = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);
   202→        throw new Error(
   203→          `Bifrost LLM gateway unreachable at ${apiBase}: ${reason}. ` +
   204→          `Check that BIFROST_BASE_URL is reachable from inside the container.`
   205→        );
   206→      }
   207→    }
   208→
   209→    // Convert agents payload from the backend into SDK AgentDefinition objects.
   210→    // The SDK only accepts 'sonnet' | 'opus' | 'haiku' | 'inherit' for model.
   211→    // Arbitrary strings (e.g. "openai/gpt-5.2") are silently rejected, causing
   212→    // the agent to not be registered.  Validate and fall back to "inherit".
   213→    const VALID_AGENT_MODELS = new Set(["sonnet", "opus", "haiku", "inherit"]);
   214→    let sdkAgents: Record<string, AgentDefinition> | undefined;
   215→    if (body.agents) {
   216→      sdkAgents = {};
   217→      for (const [name, def] of Object.entries(body.agents)) {
   218→        const model = def.model && VALID_AGENT_MODELS.has(def.model)
   219→          ? (def.model as AgentDefinition["model"])
   220→          : "inherit";
   221→        sdkAgents[name] = {
   222→          description: def.description,
   223→          prompt: def.prompt,
   224→          ...(def.tools ? { tools: def.tools } : {}),
   225→          model,
   226→        };
   227→      }
   228→    }
   229→
   230→    // Append skill restriction so the model doesn't list or suggest
   231→    // built-in skills that the PreToolUse hook would block anyway.
   232→    // The CLI binary injects its own system reminder listing ALL skills
   233→    // (including built-ins like "simplify"), so we must explicitly tell
   234→    // the model to ignore any skills not in our allowlist.
   235→    const allowedList = [...ALLOWED_SKILLS].join(", ");
   236→    const skillRestriction = ALLOWED_SKILLS.size > 0
   237→      ? `\n\nCRITICAL SKILL RESTRICTION: Your ONLY available skills are: ${allowedList}. You may see other skills (like "simplify") listed in system reminders — those are NOT available to you and MUST be ignored. When asked about available skills, list ONLY: ${allowedList}. Never mention, suggest, or attempt to invoke any skill not in this list.`
   238→      : "\n\nCRITICAL SKILL RESTRICTION: You have NO skills available. You may see skills listed in system reminders — those are NOT available to you and MUST be ignored. Never mention, suggest, or attempt to invoke any skills.";
   239→
   240→    // Common SDK options (without resume/prompt — those vary on retry)
   241→    const baseOptions = {
   242→      model: modelName,
   243→      systemPrompt: body.system_prompt + skillRestriction,
   244→      allowedTools: ["Skill", "Task", "mcp__duckdb-data-agent__execute_sql", "mcp__duckdb-data-agent__ask_user_question", "mcp__duckdb-data-agent__render_chart"] as string[],
   245→      settingSources: ["project"] as SettingSource[],
   246→      plugins: [],
   247→      hooks: { PreToolUse: [skillAllowlistHook] },
   248→      permissionMode: "bypassPermissions" as const,
   249→      allowDangerouslySkipPermissions: true,
   250→      maxTurns: 20,
   251→      includePartialMessages: true,
   252→      abortController,
   253→      env: sdkEnv,
   254→      stderr: (line: string) => {
   255→        stderrLines.push(line);
   256→        console.error(`[sidecar:cli] ${line}`);
   257→      },
   258→      ...(sdkAgents ? { agents: sdkAgents } : {}),
   259→      ...(body.mcp_server_url
   260→        ? {
   261→            mcpServers: {
   262→              "duckdb-data-agent": {
   263→                type: "sse" as const,
   264→                url: body.mcp_server_url,
   265→              },
   266→            },
   267→          }
   268→        : {}),
   269→    };
   270→
   271→    // Resume fallback: try with resume first. If the SDK yields a "result"
   272→    // message with "No conversation found" (container was recreated and lost
   273→    // the session), suppress that error and retry without resume, prepending
   274→    // conversation history so the model has context.
   275→    let currentPrompt = body.message;
   276→    let useResume = !!body.session_id;
   277→    let retried = false;
   278→
   279→    queryLoop: for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 2; attempt++) {
   280→      const sdkQuery = query({
   281→        prompt: currentPrompt,
   282→        options: {
   283→          ...baseOptions,
   284→          ...(useResume ? { resume: body.session_id } : {}),
   285→        },
   286→      });
   287→
   288→      console.log(
   289→        `[sidecar] SDK query started model=${modelName} reqId=${requestId} attempt=${attempt} resume=${useResume}`
   290→      );
   291→
   292→      idleTimer = setTimeout(() => {
   293→        console.error(`[sidecar] SDK idle timeout (${SDK_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS}ms) reached, aborting reqId=${requestId}`);
   294→        abortController.abort();
   295→      }, SDK_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS);
   296→
   297→      let resumeFailedNeedsRetry = false;
   298→
   299→      try {
   300→        for await (const message of sdkQuery) {
   301→          // Reset idle timer on every message
   302→          if (idleTimer) { clearTimeout(idleTimer); }
   303→          idleTimer = setTimeout(() => {
   304→            console.error(`[sidecar] SDK idle timeout (${SDK_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS}ms) reached, aborting reqId=${requestId}`);
   305→            abortController.abort();
   306→          }, SDK_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS);
   307→
   308→          if (responseEnded) break queryLoop;
   309→
   310→          // Intercept "No conversation found" result before forwarding
   311→          if (useResume && !retried) {
   312→            const msg = message as Record<string, unknown>;
   313→            if (msg.type === "result" && msg.is_error) {
   314→              const resultText = String(msg.result || "");
   315→              const errors = ((msg.errors as string[]) || []).join(" ");
   316→              if ((resultText + " " + errors).includes("No conversation found")
   317→                  && body.conversation_history && body.conversation_history.length > 0) {
   318→                console.log(`[sidecar] Resume failed ("No conversation found"), will retry with history reqId=${requestId}`);
   319→                resumeFailedNeedsRetry = true;
   320→                break; // Don't forward this error to the client
   321→              }
   322→            }
   323→          }
   324→
   325→          // Forward each SDK message as an SSE data line
   326→          res.write(`data: ${JSON.stringify(message)}\n\n`);
   327→
   328→          // --- Create Langfuse observations from SDK messages ---
   329→          if (!trace) continue;
   330→          const msg = message as Record<string, unknown>;
   331→
   332→          if (msg.type === "stream_event") {
   333→            const event = (msg.event as Record<string, unknown>) || {};
   334→            const eventType = event.type as string;
   335→            if (eventType === "message_start") {
   336→              const msgData = (event.message as Record<string, unknown>) || {};
   337→              const usage = (msgData.usage as Record<string, number>) || {};
   338→              currentGenUsage = { input: usage.input_tokens || 0 };
   339→            } else if (eventType === "message_delta") {
   340→              const usage = (event.usage as Record<string, number>) || {};
   341→              currentGenUsage.output = usage.output_tokens || 0;
   342→            }
   343→          } else if (msg.type === "assistant") {
   344→            const msgObj = (msg.message as Record<string, unknown>) || {};
   345→            const content = msgObj.content as unknown[];
   346→            const usage =
   347→              currentGenUsage.input !== undefined
   348→                ? {
   349→                    input: currentGenUsage.input || 0,
   350→                    output: currentGenUsage.output || 0,
   351→                    total: (currentGenUsage.input || 0) + (currentGenUsage.output || 0),
   352→                    unit: "TOKENS" as const,
   353→                  }
   354→                : undefined;
   355→            const gen = trace.generation({
   356→              name: "claude.assistant.turn",
   357→              model: (msgObj.model as string) || modelName,
   358→              input: { messages: accumulatedMessages.slice(-6) },
   359→              output: { content, role: "assistant" },
   360→              usage,
   361→            });
   362→            gen.end();
   363→            currentGenUsage = {};
   364→            accumulatedMessages.push({ role: "assistant", content });
   365→          } else if (msg.type === "user") {
   366→            const msgObj = (msg.message as Record<string, unknown>) || {};
   367→            accumulatedMessages.push({ role: "user", content: msgObj.content });
   368→          } else if (msg.type === "result") {
   369→            const actualSessionId = (msg.session_id as string | undefined) || body.session_id;
   370→            const traceSessionId = body.langfuse_session_id || actualSessionId;
   371→            trace.update({
   372→              ...(traceSessionId ? { sessionId: traceSessionId } : {}),
   373→              output: { session_id: actualSessionId },
   374→            });
   375→          } else if (msg.type === "system") {
   376→            const sessionId = msg.session_id as string | undefined;
   377→            if (sessionId && !body.langfuse_session_id) {
   378→              trace.update({ sessionId });
   379→            }
   380→          }
   381→        } // end for-await
   382→      } catch (innerErr: unknown) {
   383→        // If we're retrying due to resume failure, swallow the cleanup error
   384→        // (e.g., "Claude Code process exited with code 1" after session not found)
   385→        if (resumeFailedNeedsRetry) {
   386→          console.log(`[sidecar] Swallowing post-resume error during retry: ${innerErr instanceof Error ? innerErr.message : String(innerErr)}`);
   387→        } else {
   388→          throw innerErr;
   389→        }
   390→      }
   391→
   392→      if (resumeFailedNeedsRetry && !retried) {
   393→        retried = true;
   394→        useResume = false;
   395→        let messageWithHistory = "Previous conversation (for context):\n";
   396→        for (const entry of body.conversation_history!) {
   397→          const role = (entry.role || "user").charAt(0).toUpperCase() + (entry.role || "user").slice(1);
   398→          messageWithHistory += `\n${role}: ${entry.content}\n`;
   399→        }
   400→        messageWithHistory += `\n---\n\nMy new message:\n${body.message}`;
   401→        currentPrompt = messageWithHistory;
   402→        stderrLines.length = 0;
   403→        if (idleTimer) { clearTimeout(idleTimer); idleTimer = null; }
   404→        continue; // retry the queryLoop
   405→      }
   406→
   407→      break; // success or non-retryable error — exit queryLoop
   408→    } // end queryLoop
   409→  } catch (err: unknown) {
   410→    let errMsg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
   411→    // Append CLI stderr for context when the process crashes
   412→    if (stderrLines.length > 0) {
   413→      errMsg += ` | CLI stderr: ${stderrLines.slice(-5).join(" ")}`;
   414→    }
   415→    // Don't log abort errors — they are expected on client disconnect
   416→    if (!(err instanceof Error && err.name === "AbortError")) {
   417→      console.error(`[sidecar] SDK error: ${errMsg}`);
   418→      if (!responseEnded) {
   419→        res.write(
   420→          `data: ${JSON.stringify({ type: "error", message: errMsg })}\n\n`
   421→        );
   422→      }
   423→      if (trace) {
   424→        trace.update({ output: { error: errMsg } });
   425→      }
   426→    }
   427→  } finally {
   428→    if (idleTimer) { clearTimeout(idleTimer); }
   429→    activeAborts.delete(requestId);
   430→    responseEnded = true;
   431→    res.end();
   432→    // Flush Langfuse events before the response is fully closed
   433→    if (langfuse) {
   434→      await langfuse.flushAsync().catch(() => {});
   435→    }
   436→    console.log(`[sidecar] SSE stream ended reqId=${requestId}`);
   437→  }
   438→});
   439→
   440→app.post("/stop", (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
   441→  if (activeAborts.size > 0) {
   442→    for (const [id, controller] of activeAborts) {
   443→      controller.abort();
   444→      activeAborts.delete(id);
   445→    }
   446→    res.json({ status: "stopped" });
   447→  } else {
   448→    res.json({ status: "no_active_session" });
   449→  }
   450→});
   451→
   452→app.listen(PORT, "0.0.0.0", () => {
   453→  console.log(`Sidecar agent server listening on port ${PORT}`);
   454→});
   455→

     1→import type { ToolCallResult, UserQuestionData, UserQuestionOption } from '../types';
     2→
     3→interface AgentCallbacks {
     4→  onTextChunk: (text: string) => void;
     5→  onThinkingDone: () => void;
     6→  onToolCall: (pending: ToolCallResult) => void;
     7→  onToolResult: (result: ToolCallResult) => void;
     8→  onSubagentStart?: (data: { id: string; name: string; prompt: string }) => void;
     9→  onSubagentEnd?: (data: { id: string; name: string; result?: string; sql_results?: Array<{ sql: string; columns?: string[]; rows?: Record<string, unknown>[]; rowCount?: number; error?: string }>; chart_spec?: { data: unknown[]; layout?: Record<string, unknown> } }) => void;
    10→  onDone: (sessionId: string | null) => void;
    11→  onError: (error: string) => void;
    12→  onUserQuestion?: (data: UserQuestionData) => void;
    13→}
    14→
    15→export type { AgentCallbacks };
    16→
    17→async function streamSSE(
    18→  response: Response,
    19→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
    20→  signal?: AbortSignal,
    21→): Promise<void> {
    22→  const reader = response.body?.getReader();
    23→  if (!reader) {
    24→    callbacks.onError('No response stream');
    25→    return;
    26→  }
    27→
    28→  let doneReceived = false;
    29→  const wrappedCallbacks: AgentCallbacks = {
    30→    ...callbacks,
    31→    onDone: (sessionId) => {
    32→      doneReceived = true;
    33→      callbacks.onDone(sessionId);
    34→    },
    35→    onError: (error) => {
    36→      doneReceived = true;
    37→      callbacks.onError(error);
    38→    },
    39→  };
    40→
    41→  try {
    42→    const decoder = new TextDecoder();
    43→    let buffer = '';
    44→    let eventType = '';
    45→
    46→    while (true) {
    47→      const { done, value } = await reader.read();
    48→      if (done) break;
    49→
    50→      buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
    51→      const lines = buffer.split('\n');
    52→      buffer = lines.pop() ?? '';
    53→      for (const line of lines) {
    54→        if (line.startsWith('event: ')) {
    55→          eventType = line.slice(7).trim();
    56→        } else if (line.startsWith('data: ')) {
    57→          const data = line.slice(6);
    58→          try {
    59→            const parsed = JSON.parse(data);
    60→            handleSSEEvent(eventType, parsed, wrappedCallbacks);
    61→          } catch {
    62→            // Skip malformed JSON
    63→          }
    64→          eventType = '';
    65→        }
    66→      }
    67→    }
    68→
    69→    // Process any remaining data left in buffer after stream ends
    70→    if (buffer.trim()) {
    71→      const remainingLines = buffer.split('\n');
    72→      for (const line of remainingLines) {
    73→        if (line.startsWith('event: ')) {
    74→          eventType = line.slice(7).trim();
    75→        } else if (line.startsWith('data: ')) {
    76→          const data = line.slice(6);
    77→          try {
    78→            const parsed = JSON.parse(data);
    79→            handleSSEEvent(eventType, parsed, wrappedCallbacks);
    80→          } catch {
    81→            // Skip malformed JSON
    82→          }
    83→          eventType = '';
    84→        }
    85→      }
    86→    }
    87→
    88→    // Safety net: if stream ended without done/error, force done
    89→    if (!doneReceived) {
    90→      callbacks.onDone(null);
    91→    }
    92→  } catch (e: unknown) {
    93→    if (signal?.aborted) return;
    94→    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Connection failed';
    95→    callbacks.onError(msg);
    96→  }
    97→}
    98→
    99→export async function runAgentLoop(
   100→  message: string,
   101→  agentSessionId: string | null,
   102→  langfuseSessionId: string | null,
   103→  conversationHistory: { role: string; content: string }[] | null,
   104→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
   105→  signal?: AbortSignal,
   106→  userSessionId?: string,
   107→): Promise<void> {
   108→  try {
   109→    const response = await fetch('/api/chat', {
   110→      method: 'POST',
   111→      headers: {
   112→        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   113→        ...(userSessionId ? { 'X-Session-ID': userSessionId } : {}),
   114→      },
   115→      body: JSON.stringify({
   116→        message,
   117→        session_id: agentSessionId,
   118→        langfuse_session_id: langfuseSessionId,
   119→        conversation_history: conversationHistory ?? [],
   120→      }),
   121→      signal,
   122→    });
   123→
   124→    if (!response.ok) {
   125→      const errorText = await response.text();
   126→      callbacks.onError(`Server error: ${errorText}`);
   127→      return;
   128→    }
   129→
   130→    await streamSSE(response, callbacks, signal);
   131→  } catch (e: unknown) {
   132→    if (signal?.aborted) return;
   133→    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Connection failed';
   134→    callbacks.onError(msg);
   135→  }
   136→}
   137→
   138→export async function runAgentEditLoop(
   139→  newMessage: string,
   140→  conversationHistory: { role: string; content: string }[],
   141→  langfuseSessionId: string | null,
   142→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
   143→  signal?: AbortSignal,
   144→  userSessionId?: string,
   145→): Promise<void> {
   146→  try {
   147→    const response = await fetch('/api/chat/edit', {
   148→      method: 'POST',
   149→      headers: {
   150→        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   151→        ...(userSessionId ? { 'X-Session-ID': userSessionId } : {}),
   152→      },
   153→      body: JSON.stringify({
   154→        new_message: newMessage,
   155→        conversation_history: conversationHistory,
   156→        langfuse_session_id: langfuseSessionId,
   157→      }),
   158→      signal,
   159→    });
   160→
   161→    if (!response.ok) {
   162→      const errorText = await response.text();
   163→      callbacks.onError(`Server error: ${errorText}`);
   164→      return;
   165→    }
   166→
   167→    await streamSSE(response, callbacks, signal);
   168→  } catch (e: unknown) {
   169→    if (signal?.aborted) return;
   170→    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Connection failed';
   171→    callbacks.onError(msg);
   172→  }
   173→}
   174→
   175→function handleSSEEvent(
   176→  eventType: string,
   177→  data: Record<string, unknown>,
   178→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
   179→) {
   180→  switch (eventType) {
   181→    case 'thinking':
   182→    case 'answer':
   183→      callbacks.onTextChunk(data.text as string);
   184→      break;
   185→    case 'thinking_done':
   186→      callbacks.onThinkingDone();
   187→      break;
   188→    case 'tool_call':
   189→      callbacks.onToolCall({
   190→        toolCallId: (data.id as string) ?? '',
   191→        toolName: (data.name as string) ?? undefined,
   192→        sql: (data.sql as string) ?? '',
   193→        command: (data.command as string) ?? undefined,
   194→        toolInput: (data.input as Record<string, unknown>) ?? undefined,
   195→        columns: [],
   196→        rows: [],
   197→        rowCount: 0,
   198→      });
   199→      break;
   200→    case 'tool_result': {
   201→      const result: ToolCallResult = {
   202→        toolCallId: (data.id as string) ?? '',
   203→        toolName: (data.name as string) ?? undefined,
   204→        sql: (data.sql as string) ?? '',
   205→        columns: (data.columns as string[]) ?? [],
   206→        rows: (data.rows as Record<string, unknown>[]) ?? [],
   207→        rowCount: (data.rowCount as number) ?? 0,
   208→        error: (data.error as string) ?? undefined,
   209→        output: (data.output as string) ?? undefined,
   210→        rawContent: (data.content as string) ?? undefined,
   211→        chart_spec: (data.chart_spec as { data: unknown[]; layout?: Record<string, unknown> }) ?? undefined,
   212→      };
   213→      callbacks.onToolResult(result);
   214→      break;
   215→    }
   216→    case 'subagent_start':
   217→      callbacks.onSubagentStart?.({
   218→        id: data.id as string,
   219→        name: data.name as string,
   220→        prompt: (data.prompt as string) ?? '',
   221→      });
   222→      break;
   223→    case 'subagent_end': {
   224→      type ChartSpec = { data: unknown[]; layout?: Record<string, unknown>; frames?: unknown[] };
   225→      // Backend sends chart_specs (array) for multi-chart support.
   226→      // Fall back to legacy chart_spec (single object) for backwards compat.
   227→      const rawSpecs = data.chart_specs as ChartSpec[] | undefined;
   228→      const legacySpec = data.chart_spec as ChartSpec | undefined;
   229→      const chartSpecs: ChartSpec[] = rawSpecs ?? (legacySpec ? [legacySpec] : []);
   230→
   231→      if (chartSpecs.length > 0) {
   232→        // Emit one callback per chart so each gets its own segment
   233→        for (const spec of chartSpecs) {
   234→          callbacks.onSubagentEnd?.({
   235→            id: data.id as string,
   236→            name: data.name as string,
   237→            chart_spec: spec,
   238→          });
   239→        }
   240→        // Emit text result separately (if any) so it doesn't get lost
   241→        if (data.result) {
   242→          callbacks.onSubagentEnd?.({
   243→            id: data.id as string,
   244→            name: data.name as string,
   245→            result: data.result as string,
   246→          });
   247→        }
   248→      } else {
   249→        callbacks.onSubagentEnd?.({
   250→          id: data.id as string,
   251→          name: data.name as string,
   252→          result: (data.result as string) ?? undefined,
   253→          sql_results: (data.sql_results as Array<{ sql: string; columns?: string[]; rows?: Record<string, unknown>[]; rowCount?: number; error?: string }>) ?? undefined,
   254→        });
   255→      }
   256→      break;
   257→    }
   258→    case 'user_question': {
   259→      // Normalize options: the agent may send plain strings or {label, description} objects
   260→      const rawOptions = (data.options as unknown[]) ?? [];
   261→      const normalizedOptions: UserQuestionOption[] = (Array.isArray(rawOptions) ? rawOptions : []).map(
   262→        (opt: unknown) => {
   263→          if (typeof opt === 'string') return { label: opt };
   264→          if (typeof opt === 'object' && opt !== null && 'label' in opt) return opt as UserQuestionOption;
   265→          return { label: String(opt) };
   266→        }
   267→      );
   268→      callbacks.onUserQuestion?.({
   269→        questionId: data.question_id as string,
   270→        question: data.question as string,
   271→        options: normalizedOptions,
   272→        multiSelect: (data.multi_select as boolean) ?? false,
   273→      });
   274→      break;
   275→    }
   276→    case 'done':
   277→      callbacks.onDone((data.session_id as string) ?? null);
   278→      break;
   279→    case 'error':
   280→      callbacks.onError((data.message as string) ?? 'Unknown error');
   281→      break;
   282→  }
   283→}
   284→
```

     1→import {
     2→  useCallback,
     3→  useRef,
     4→  useState,
     5→  type ReactNode,
     6→} from 'react';
     7→import { AgentContext } from '../hooks/useAgent';
     8→import { runAgentLoop, runAgentEditLoop } from '../agent/agentService';
     9→import type { ChatMessage, ContentSegment, ToolCallResult } from '../types';
    10→import { useSessionId } from '../hooks/useSessionId';
    11→import { generateUUID } from '../utils/uuid';
    12→
    13→function generateId() {
    14→  return generateUUID();
    15→}
    16→
    17→export function AgentProvider({
    18→  children,
    19→  refreshTables,
    20→}: {
    21→  children: ReactNode;
    22→  refreshTables: () => Promise<void>;
    23→}) {
    24→  const userSessionId = useSessionId();
    25→  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<ChatMessage[]>([]);
    26→  const [isStreaming, setIsStreaming] = useState(false);
    27→  const abortRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);
    28→  const textBufferRef = useRef('');
    29→  const flushTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
    30→  const assistantIdRef = useRef('');
    31→  const segmentsRef = useRef<ContentSegment[]>([]);
    32→  const currentTextRef = useRef('');
    33→  const phaseRef = useRef<'thinking' | 'answer'>('thinking');
    34→  const sessionIdRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
    35→  const pendingHistoryRef = useRef<{ role: string; content: string }[] | null>(null);
    36→
    37→  const flushText = useCallback(() => {
    38→    const text = textBufferRef.current;
    39→    if (!text) return;
    40→    const id = assistantIdRef.current;
    41→    currentTextRef.current += text;
    42→    setMessages((prev) =>
    43→      prev.map((m) =>
    44→        m.id === id ? { ...m, content: m.content + text } : m
    45→      )
    46→    );
    47→    textBufferRef.current = '';
    48→  }, []);
    49→
    50→  const sendMessage = useCallback(
    51→    async (text: string) => {
    52→      if (isStreaming) return;
    53→
    54→      const userMsg: ChatMessage = {
    55→        id: generateId(),
    56→        role: 'user',
    57→        content: text,
    58→      };
    59→
    60→      const assistantId = generateId();
    61→      assistantIdRef.current = assistantId;
    62→      const assistantMsg: ChatMessage = {
    63→        id: assistantId,
    64→        role: 'assistant',
    65→        content: '',
    66→        toolCalls: [],
    67→        isStreaming: true,
    68→      };
    69→
    70→      setMessages((prev) => [...prev, userMsg, assistantMsg]);
    71→      setIsStreaming(true);
    72→      textBufferRef.current = '';
    73→      segmentsRef.current = [];
    74→      currentTextRef.current = '';
    75→      phaseRef.current = 'thinking';
    76→
    77→      // Build conversation history from current messages for resume fallback
    78→      const history = messages
    79→        .filter((m) => m.role === 'user' || m.role === 'assistant')
    80→        .map((m) => ({ role: m.role, content: m.content }));
    81→
    82→      // If there's pending history from a delete, start a new Langfuse session with that context
    83→      const pendingHistory = pendingHistoryRef.current;
    84→      pendingHistoryRef.current = null;
    85→      const langfuseSessionId = pendingHistory ? generateUUID() : null;
    86→
    87→      const controller = new AbortController();
    88→      abortRef.current = controller;
    89→
    90→      await runAgentLoop(
    91→        text,
    92→        sessionIdRef.current,
    93→        langfuseSessionId,
    94→        pendingHistory ?? (history.length > 0 ? history : null),
    95→        {
    96→          onTextChunk: (chunk) => {
    97→            textBufferRef.current += chunk;
    98→            if (!flushTimerRef.current) {
    99→              flushTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
   100→                flushText();
   101→                flushTimerRef.current = null;
   102→              }, 50);
   103→            }
   104→          },
   105→          onThinkingDone: () => {
   106→            // Extended thinking just ended and a text block is starting.
   107→            // Create a thinking segment from accumulated thinking text.
   108→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   109→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   110→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   111→            }
   112→            flushText();
   113→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   114→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'thinking', text: currentTextRef.current });
   115→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   116→            }
   117→            phaseRef.current = 'answer';
   118→            setMessages((prev) =>
   119→              prev.map((m) =>
   120→                m.id === assistantId
   121→                  ? { ...m, currentPhase: 'answer', segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   122→                  : m
   123→              )
   124→            );
   125→          },
   126→          onToolCall: (pending: ToolCallResult) => {
   127→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   128→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   129→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   130→            }
   131→            flushText();
   132→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   133→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   134→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   135→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   136→            }
   137→            // Add a pending tool segment so the input is shown immediately
   138→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   139→              type: 'tool',
   140→              toolResult: pending,
   141→            });
   142→            setMessages((prev) =>
   143→              prev.map((m) =>
   144→                m.id === assistantId
   145→                  ? { ...m, currentPhase: 'answer', segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   146→                  : m
   147→              )
   148→            );
   149→          },
   150→          onToolResult: (result: ToolCallResult) => {
   151→            // Merge result into pending tool segment (keep input info, add output)
   152→            const pendingIdx = segmentsRef.current.findIndex(
   153→              (s) => s.type === 'tool' && s.toolResult?.toolCallId === result.toolCallId
   154→            );
   155→            if (pendingIdx !== -1) {
   156→              const pending = segmentsRef.current[pendingIdx].toolResult!;
   157→              segmentsRef.current[pendingIdx] = {
   158→                type: 'tool',
   159→                toolResult: {
   160→                  ...pending,
   161→                  ...result,
   162→                  sql: result.sql || pending.sql,
   163→                  toolName: result.toolName || pending.toolName,
   164→                  command: result.command || pending.command,
   165→                  toolInput: result.toolInput || pending.toolInput,
   166→                },
   167→              };
   168→            } else {
   169→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'tool', toolResult: result });
   170→            }
   171→            setMessages((prev) =>
   172→              prev.map((m) =>
   173→                m.id === assistantId
   174→                  ? { ...m, toolCalls: [...(m.toolCalls || []), result], segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   175→                  : m
   176→              )
   177→            );
   178→            refreshTables();
   179→          },
   180→          onSubagentStart: (data) => {
   181→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   182→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   183→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   184→            }
   185→            flushText();
   186→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   187→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   188→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   189→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   190→            }
   191→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   192→              type: 'subagent_start',
   193→              subagentId: data.id,
   194→              subagentName: data.name,
   195→              text: data.prompt,
   196→            });
   197→            setMessages((prev) =>
   198→              prev.map((m) =>
   199→                m.id === assistantId
   200→                  ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   201→                  : m
   202→              )
   203→            );
   204→          },
   205→          onSubagentEnd: (data) => {
   206→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   207→              type: 'subagent_end',
   208→              subagentId: data.id,
   209→              subagentName: data.name,
   210→              chart_spec: data.chart_spec,
   211→              sqlResults: data.sql_results,

   214→            setMessages((prev) =>

   220→            );
   221→          },
   222→          onDone: (newSessionId) => {
   223→            if (newSessionId) sessionIdRef.current = newSessionId;
   224→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   225→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   226→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   227→            }
   228→            flushText();
   229→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {

   236→            setMessages((prev) =>

   239→                  ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, currentPhase: undefined, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }

   242→            );
   243→            setIsStreaming(false);
   244→            abortRef.current = null;
   245→          },
   246→          onError: (error) => {
   247→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   248→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   249→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   250→            }
   251→            flushText();
   252→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   253→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   254→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   255→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   256→            }
   257→            segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'error', errorMessage: error });
   258→            setMessages((prev) =>
   259→              prev.map((m) =>
   260→                m.id === assistantId
   261→                  ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, currentPhase: undefined, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   262→                  : m
   263→              )
   264→            );
   265→            setIsStreaming(false);
   266→            abortRef.current = null;
   267→          },
   268→          onUserQuestion: (data) => {
   269→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   270→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   271→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   272→            }
   273→            flushText();
   274→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   275→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   276→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   277→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   278→            }
   279→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   280→              type: 'user_question',
   281→              questionData: data,
   282→            });
   283→            setMessages((prev) =>
   284→              prev.map((m) =>
   285→                m.id === assistantId

   295→    },
   296→    [isStreaming, flushText, refreshTables, userSessionId]
   297→  );
   298→
   299→  const editMessage = useCallback(
   300→    async (messageIndex: number, newContent: string) => {
   301→      if (isStreaming) return;
   302→
   303→      // Build conversation history from messages before the edit point
   304→      const conversationHistory: { role: string; content: string }[] = [];
   305→      for (let i = 0; i < messageIndex; i++) {
   306→        const msg = messages[i];
   307→        if (msg.role === 'user' || msg.role === 'assistant') {
   308→          conversationHistory.push({ role: msg.role, content: msg.content });
   309→        }
   310→      }
   311→
   312→      const assistantId = generateId();
   313→      assistantIdRef.current = assistantId;
   314→
   315→      const userMsg: ChatMessage = {
   316→        id: generateId(),
   317→        role: 'user',
   318→        content: newContent,
   319→      };
   320→      const assistantMsg: ChatMessage = {
   321→        id: assistantId,
   322→        role: 'assistant',
   323→        content: '',
   324→        toolCalls: [],
   325→        isStreaming: true,
   326→      };
   327→
   328→      setMessages((prev) => [...prev.slice(0, messageIndex), userMsg, assistantMsg]);
   329→      setIsStreaming(true);
   330→      textBufferRef.current = '';
   331→      segmentsRef.current = [];
   332→      currentTextRef.current = '';
   333→      phaseRef.current = 'thinking';
   334→
   335→      const controller = new AbortController();
   336→      abortRef.current = controller;
   337→
   338→      await runAgentEditLoop(
   339→        newContent,
   340→        conversationHistory,
   341→        generateUUID(),
   342→        {
   343→          onTextChunk: (chunk) => {
   344→            textBufferRef.current += chunk;
   345→            if (!flushTimerRef.current) {
   346→              flushTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
   347→                flushText();
   348→                flushTimerRef.current = null;
   349→              }, 50);
   350→            }
   351→          },
   352→          onThinkingDone: () => {
   353→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   354→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   355→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   356→            }
   357→            flushText();
   358→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   359→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'thinking', text: currentTextRef.current });
   360→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   361→            }
   362→            phaseRef.current = 'answer';
   363→            setMessages((prev) =>
   364→              prev.map((m) =>
   365→                m.id === assistantId
   366→                  ? { ...m, currentPhase: 'answer', segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   367→                  : m
   368→              )
   369→            );
   370→          },
   371→          onToolCall: (pending: ToolCallResult) => {
   372→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   373→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   374→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   375→            }
   376→            flushText();
   377→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   378→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   379→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   380→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   381→            }
   382→            segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'tool', toolResult: pending });
   383→            setMessages((prev) =>
   384→              prev.map((m) =>
   385→                m.id === assistantId
   386→                  ? { ...m, currentPhase: 'answer', segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   387→                  : m
   388→              )
   389→            );
   390→          },
   391→          onToolResult: (result: ToolCallResult) => {
   392→            const pendingIdx = segmentsRef.current.findIndex(
   393→              (s) => s.type === 'tool' && s.toolResult?.toolCallId === result.toolCallId
   394→            );
   395→            if (pendingIdx !== -1) {
   396→              const pending = segmentsRef.current[pendingIdx].toolResult!;
   397→              segmentsRef.current[pendingIdx] = {
   398→                type: 'tool',
   399→                toolResult: {
   400→                  ...pending,
   401→                  ...result,
   402→                  sql: result.sql || pending.sql,
   403→                  toolName: result.toolName || pending.toolName,
   404→                  command: result.command || pending.command,
   405→                  toolInput: result.toolInput || pending.toolInput,
   406→                },
   407→              };
   408→            } else {
   409→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'tool', toolResult: result });
   410→            }
   411→            setMessages((prev) =>
   412→              prev.map((m) =>
   413→                m.id === assistantId
   414→                  ? { ...m, toolCalls: [...(m.toolCalls || []), result], segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   415→                  : m
   416→              )
   417→            );
   418→            refreshTables();
   419→          },
   420→          onSubagentStart: (data) => {
   421→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   422→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   423→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   424→            }
   425→            flushText();
   426→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   427→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   428→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   429→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   430→            }
   431→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   432→              type: 'subagent_start',
   433→              subagentId: data.id,
   434→              subagentName: data.name,
   435→              text: data.prompt,
   436→            });
   437→            setMessages((prev) =>
   438→              prev.map((m) =>
   439→                m.id === assistantId
   440→                  ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   441→                  : m
   442→              )
   443→            );
   444→          },
   445→          onSubagentEnd: (data) => {
   446→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   447→              type: 'subagent_end',
   448→              subagentId: data.id,
   449→              subagentName: data.name,
   450→              chart_spec: data.chart_spec,
   451→              sqlResults: data.sql_results,
   452→              text: data.result,
   453→            });
   454→            setMessages((prev) =>
   455→              prev.map((m) =>
   456→                m.id === assistantId
   457→                  ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   458→                  : m
   459→              )
   460→            );
   461→          },
   462→          onDone: (newSessionId) => {
   463→            if (newSessionId) sessionIdRef.current = newSessionId;
   464→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   465→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   466→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   467→            }
   468→            flushText();
   469→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   470→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'answer', text: currentTextRef.current });
   471→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   472→            }
   473→            setMessages((prev) =>
   474→              prev.map((m) =>
   475→                m.id === assistantId
   476→                  ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, currentPhase: undefined, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   477→                  : m
   478→              )
   479→            );
   480→            setIsStreaming(false);
   481→            abortRef.current = null;
   482→          },
   483→          onError: (error) => {
   484→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   485→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   486→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   487→            }
   488→            flushText();
   489→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   490→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   491→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   492→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   493→            }
   494→            segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'error', errorMessage: error });
   495→            setMessages((prev) =>
   496→              prev.map((m) =>
   497→                m.id === assistantId
   498→                  ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, currentPhase: undefined, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   499→                  : m
   500→              )
   501→            );
   502→            setIsStreaming(false);
   503→            abortRef.current = null;
   504→          },
   505→          onUserQuestion: (data) => {
   506→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   507→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   508→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   509→            }
   510→            flushText();
   511→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   512→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   513→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   514→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   515→            }
   516→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   517→              type: 'user_question',
   518→              questionData: data,
   519→            });
   520→            setMessages((prev) =>
   521→              prev.map((m) =>
   522→                m.id === assistantId
   523→                  ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   524→                  : m
   525→              )
   526→            );
   527→          },
   528→        },
   529→        controller.signal,
   530→        userSessionId,
   531→      );
   532→    },
   533→    [isStreaming, messages, flushText, refreshTables, userSessionId]
   534→  );
   535→
   536→  const deleteMessage = useCallback(
   537→    (messageIndex: number) => {
   538→      if (isStreaming) return;
   539→
   540→      // Store remaining messages as history for Langfuse context on next send
   541→      setMessages((prev) => {
   542→        const remaining = prev.slice(0, messageIndex);
   543→        pendingHistoryRef.current = remaining
   544→          .filter((m) => m.role === 'user' || m.role === 'assistant')
   545→          .map((m) => ({ role: m.role, content: m.content }));
   546→        return remaining;
   547→      });
   548→
   549→      // Clear session so next message starts fresh
   550→      sessionIdRef.current = null;
   551→    },
   552→    [isStreaming]
   553→  );
   554→
   555→  const respondToQuestion = useCallback(
   556→    async (questionId: string, answers: string[], freeText?: string) => {
   557→      // Update the segment to show the user's answer
   558→      const segIdx = segmentsRef.current.findIndex(
   559→        (s) => s.type === 'user_question' && s.questionData?.questionId === questionId
   560→      );
   561→      if (segIdx !== -1) {
   562→        segmentsRef.current[segIdx] = {
   563→          ...segmentsRef.current[segIdx],
   564→          userAnswer: answers,
   565→          userFreeText: freeText,
   566→        };
   567→        setMessages((prev) =>
   568→          prev.map((m) =>
   569→            m.id === assistantIdRef.current
   570→              ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   571→              : m
   572→          )
   573→        );
   574→      }
   575→
   576→      // POST to backend
   577→      try {
   578→        await fetch('/api/chat/respond', {
   579→          method: 'POST',
   580→          headers: {
   581→            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   582→            ...(userSessionId ? { 'X-Session-ID': userSessionId } : {}),
   583→          },
   584→          body: JSON.stringify({
   585→            question_id: questionId,
   586→            answers,
   587→            free_text: freeText || null,
   588→          }),
   589→        });
   590→      } catch (e) {
   591→        console.error('Failed to respond to question:', e);
   592→      }
   593→    },
   594→    [userSessionId]
   595→  );
   596→
   597→  const clearMessages = useCallback(() => {
   598→    if (abortRef.current) {
   599→      abortRef.current.abort();
   600→    }
   601→    setMessages([]);
   602→    setIsStreaming(false);
   603→    sessionIdRef.current = null;
   604→    pendingHistoryRef.current = null;
   605→  }, []);
   606→
   607→  return (
   608→    <AgentContext.Provider
   609→      value={{ messages, isStreaming, sendMessage, editMessage, deleteMessage, clearMessages, respondToQuestion }}
   610→    >
   611→      {children}
   612→    </AgentContext.Provider>
   613→  );
   614→}
   615→

    20→def build_system_prompt(db: Database) -> str:
    21→    tables = db.list_tables()
    22→    prompt = """You are a helpful data analyst assistant working with a DuckDB database.
    23→
    24→Tools at your disposal:
    25→- mcp__duckdb-data-agent__execute_sql — run SQL queries against DuckDB
    26→- mcp__duckdb-data-agent__render_chart — render a Plotly chart
    27→- mcp__duckdb-data-agent__ask_user_question — ask the user a clarifying question
    28→- Task tool with subagent_type "sql-analyst" — delegate complex multi-query data exploration
    29→
    30→Task tool usage:
    31→- When using the Task tool, set "subagent_type" to "sql-analyst". NEVER use generic values like "general-purpose" or any other value.
    32→- Do NOT set the "model" parameter on the Task tool — it is already configured.
    33→- After the sql-analyst returns, do NOT repeat the data tables, numbers, or SQL queries. Simply add brief commentary on what the data means. Never include SQL in your answer unless the user explicitly asks to see the SQL.
    34→
    35→Direct tool usage:
    36→- For simple SQL queries, call execute_sql directly instead of delegating to sql-analyst.
    37→- For charts/visualizations, call execute_sql to get the data, then call render_chart yourself with the Plotly spec. Do NOT delegate charting to a subagent.
    38→
    39→Charting workflow (follow this exactly):
    40→1. Run execute_sql to get the data you need for a chart.
    41→2. Call render_chart with TWO required parameters:
    42→   - `data`: array of Plotly trace objects (e.g. [{"type": "bar", "x": [...], "y": [...]}])
    43→   - `layout`: object that MUST include `title` (e.g. {"title": "My Chart"})
    44→   Both `data` and `layout` are required — the tool WILL accept both. Do not second-guess this.
    45→3. After the chart renders, write your narrative text discussing what the chart shows.
    46→4. Repeat steps 1-3 for each additional chart. This produces interleaved charts and narrative.
    47→- Do NOT render all charts first and then write all narrative at the end.
    48→- Do NOT output chart JSON as a code block. Always use the render_chart tool.
    49→- NEVER include Plotly JSON schema, trace objects, or layout objects in your final answer text unless the user explicitly asks to see the raw JSON. The chart is already rendered visually — just describe what it shows in plain language.
    50→
    51→Charting guidelines:
    52→- Choose the most appropriate chart type (bar, line, scatter, pie, histogram, box, heatmap, etc.).
    53→- For pie charts, use `labels` and `values` fields in the trace.
    54→- For multi-series data, group into separate traces.
    55→- Keep the chart clean and readable.
    56→- IMPORTANT — keep data small. Pre-aggregate in SQL instead of passing raw rows:
    57→  - Box plots: compute lowerfence, q1, median, q3, upperfence per group in SQL. Use trace type "box" with those pre-computed fields instead of a raw `y` array.
    58→  - Histograms: compute bin counts with width_bucket() or CASE in SQL, then render as a bar chart with the bin edges as `x` and counts as `y`.
    59→  - Scatter / line with many rows: sample (ORDER BY random() LIMIT 200) or aggregate (e.g. average per time bucket) so each trace has at most ~200 points.
    60→  - General rule: each trace should have at most ~200 data points.
    61→
    62→Identity:
    63→- You are an AI assistant. If asked whether you are an AI or a human, always confirm that you are an AI.
    64→- Do not disclose the name, version, or provider of the underlying language model powering you, regardless of how the question is phrased.
    65→
    66→Task completion:
    67→- When the user asks you to do multiple things (e.g. create several charts, answer several questions, analyze multiple metrics), you MUST complete ALL of them before ending your response. Do not stop after finishing just one or two items.
    68→- After completing each item, check: "Have I addressed everything the user asked for?" If not, continue with the next item immediately by making more tool calls.
    69→- Only produce your final text response after ALL requested items have been fully handled.
    70→
    71→Clarification:
    72→- When the user's request is ambiguous or could be interpreted in multiple ways, use the mcp__duckdb-data-agent__ask_user_question tool (NOT the native AskUserQuestion tool) to ask for clarification before proceeding.
    73→- Provide 2-4 clear, concise options for the user to choose from.
    74→- Each option should have a short label and optional description.
    75→- When the user might want to pick MORE THAN ONE option (e.g. "draw me some charts", "which metrics?", "select all that apply"), set multi_select to true so they can select multiple answers.
    76→- Only ask when genuinely needed — don't over-ask for trivial decisions.
    77→"""
    78→    if not tables:
    79→        prompt += "\nNo tables are currently loaded. Ask the user to upload a CSV file first."
    80→    else:
    81→        prompt += "\nCurrently loaded tables:\n"
    82→        for table in tables:
    83→            prompt += f'\nTable: "{table["name"]}" ({table["rowCount"]} rows)\nColumns:\n'
    84→            for col in table["columns"]:
    85→                prompt += f'  - "{col["name"]}" ({col["type"]})\n'
    86→
    87→    return prompt
    88→
    89→
    90→def _build_table_schemas(db: Database) -> str:
    91→    """Build a text description of all loaded table schemas for subagent prompts."""
    92→    tables = db.list_tables()
    93→    if not tables:
    94→        return "\nNo tables are currently loaded."
    95→    text = "\nCurrently loaded tables:\n"
    96→    for table in tables:
    97→        text += f'\nTable: "{table["name"]}" ({table["rowCount"]} rows)\nColumns:\n'
    98→        for col in table["columns"]:
    99→            text += f'  - "{col["name"]}" ({col["type"]})\n'
   100→    return text

   114→        "- Use double quotes for table and column identifiers that might conflict "
   115→        "with reserved words.\n"

   127→                "dataset, multi-step aggregations, or debugging query errors. "

   135→
   136→
   137→def _build_message_with_history(
   138→    message: str, conversation_history: list[dict] | None = None
   139→) -> str:
   140→    """Prepend conversation history context to the user message when editing."""
   141→    if not conversation_history:
   142→        return message
   143→
   144→    history_text = "Previous conversation (for context, I am now editing a message):\n"
   145→    for entry in conversation_history:
   146→        role = entry.get("role", "user").capitalize()
   147→        content = entry.get("content", "")
   148→        history_text += f"\n{role}: {content}\n"
   149→    history_text += f"\n---\n\nMy updated message:\n{message}"
   150→    return history_text
   151→
   152→
   153→
   154→async def stream_chat(
   155→    message: str,
   156→    session_id: str | None = None,
   157→    db: Database | None = None,
   158→    conversation_history: list[dict] | None = None,
   159→    langfuse_session_id: str | None = None,
   160→    backend_session_id: str | None = None,
   161→) -> AsyncIterator[str]:
   162→    """Stream agent chat responses as SSE events via containerized sidecar."""
   163→    from app.container_manager import container_manager
   164→    if container_manager is None:
   165→        raise RuntimeError("Docker is not available. Container mode requires Docker.")
   166→    if db is None:
   167→        raise ValueError("db must be provided")
   168→
   169→    import httpx
   170→    import asyncio
   171→
   172→    # Only bake history into message text for edit/delete (no session to resume).
   173→    # For normal follow-ups with a session_id, pass history as a separate field
   174→    # so the sidecar can use it as fallback if resume fails.
   175→    query_message = _build_message_with_history(message, conversation_history) if not session_id else message
   176→    system_prompt = build_system_prompt(db)
   177→
   178→    # Pass Langfuse credentials to the container so the sidecar's
   179→    # TypeScript Langfuse SDK can create traces directly.
   180→    env: dict[str, str] = {
   181→        "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY": "placeholder",
   182→        "ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL": f"{BACKEND_BASE_URL}/anthropic",
   183→        "SDK_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS": str(SDK_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS),
   184→    }
   185→    if LANGFUSE_ENABLED:
   186→        env["LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY"] = LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY
   187→        env["LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY"] = LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY
   188→        env["LANGFUSE_BASE_URL"] = LANGFUSE_BASE_URL
   189→
   190→    # Use the backend session ID (X-Session-ID header) for both:
   191→    # 1. MCP SSE URL — so the container queries the correct DuckDB instance
   192→    # 2. Container lifecycle key — so the same container is reused across
   193→    #    requests from the same browser tab (the Claude agent session_id
   194→    #    changes after the first response, which would orphan the container)
   195→    stable_session = backend_session_id or session_id or "default"
   196→
   197→    try:
   198→        # Send SSE keepalive immediately so the HTTP response starts and
   199→        # intermediate proxies (Vite, nginx) don't drop the idle connection
   200→        # before we've finished the blocking Docker container creation.
   201→        yield ": keepalive\n\n"
   202→
   203→        # container_manager.create() is synchronous (blocking Docker API call).
   204→        # Run it in a thread executor so the event loop stays responsive and
   205→        # can continue flushing keepalives to the client during startup.
   206→        # gVisor (runsc) containers can take 10-30 seconds to spin up.
   207→        loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
   208→        create_future = loop.run_in_executor(None, container_manager.create, stable_session, env)
   209→
   210→        max_create_wait = 60.0
   211→        elapsed = 0.0
   212→        while not create_future.done():
   213→            await asyncio.sleep(2.0)
   214→            elapsed += 2.0
   215→            if elapsed >= max_create_wait:
   216→                create_future.cancel()
   217→                raise RuntimeError(f"Container creation timed out after {max_create_wait:.0f}s")
   218→            yield ": keepalive\n\n"
   219→        info = await create_future
   220→        container_manager.touch(stable_session)
   221→
   222→        # Wait for container to be ready
   223→        for attempt in range(10):
   224→            try:
   225→                async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=httpx.Timeout(5.0)) as check_client:
   226→                    resp = await check_client.get(f"{info.url}/health")
   227→                    if resp.status_code == 200:
   228→                        break
   229→            except Exception:
   230→                pass
   231→            yield ": keepalive\n\n"
   232→            await asyncio.sleep(1)
   233→        else:
   234→            raise RuntimeError("Sidecar container failed health check after 10 attempts")
   235→
   236→        payload: dict = {
   237→            "message": query_message,
   238→            "session_id": session_id,
   239→            "system_prompt": system_prompt,

   241→            "mcp_server_url": f"{BACKEND_BASE_URL}/mcp/sse?session_id={stable_session}",

\"# Updated\", skills_dir)\n    result = get_skill(\"analyze-data\", skills_dir)\n    assert result[\"description\"] == \"Updated desc\"\n    assert \"# Updated\" in result[\"content\"]\n\n\ndef test_update_skill_not_found(tmp_path):\n    with pytest.raises(SkillValidationError, match=\"not found\"):\n        update_skill(\"nonexistent\", \"desc\", \"content\", str(tmp_path))\n\n\ndef test_delete_skill_removes_directory(skills_dir):\n    delete_skill(\"analyze-data\", skills_dir)\n    assert list_skills(skills_dir) == []\n\n\ndef test_delete_skill_not_found(tmp_path):\n    with pytest.raises(SkillValidationError, match=\"not found\"):\n        delete_skill(\"nonexistent\", str(tmp_path))\n```\n\n**Step 2: Run tests to verify they fail**\n\nRun: `cd backend && python -m pytest tests/test_skills.py -v`\nExpected: All tests FAIL (module not found)\n\n**Step 3: Implement skill CRUD module**\n\nCreate `backend/app/skills.py`:\n\n```python\nimport os\nimport re\nimport shutil\n\n\nclass SkillValidationError(Exception):\n    pass\n\n\n_NAME_PATTERN = re.compile(r\"^[a-z0-9-]+$\")\n\n\ndef _validate_name(name: str) -> None:\n    if not name or len(name) > 64 or not _NAME_PATTERN.match(name):\n        raise SkillValidationError(\n            \"Skill name must be 1-64 characters, lowercase alphanumeric with hyphens.\"\n        )\n\n\ndef _parse_skill_md(text: str) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Parse a SKILL.md file into {name, description, content}.\"\"\"\n    if not text.startswith(\"---\"):\n        return {\"name\": \"\", \"description\": \"\", \"content\": text}\n    end = text.index(\"---\", 3)\n    frontmatter = text[3:end].strip()\n    body = text[end + 3:].strip()\n    meta: dict[str, str] = {}\n    for line in frontmatter.splitlines():\n        if \":\" in line:\n            key, _, val = line.partition(\":\")\n            meta[key.strip()] = val.strip()\n    return {\"name\": meta.get(\"name\", \"\"), \"description\": meta.get(\"description\", \"\"), \"content\": body}\n\n\ndef _write_skill_md(path: str, name: str, description: str, content: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Write a SKILL.md file with YAML frontmatter.\"\"\"\n    text = f\"---\\nname: {name}\\ndescription: {description}\\n---\\n\\n{content}\\n\"\n    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path), exist_ok=True)\n    with open(path, \"w\") as f:\n        f.write(text)\n\n\ndef list_skills(skills_dir: str) -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"List all skills (name + description only).\"\"\"\n    if not os.path.isdir(skills_dir):\n        return []\n    result = []\n    for entry in sorted(os.listdir(skills_dir)):\n        skill_path = os.path.join(skills_dir, entry, \"SKILL.md\")\n        if os.path.isdir(os.path.join(skills_dir, entry)) and os.path.isfile(skill_path):\n            with open(skill_path) as f:\n                parsed = _parse_skill_md(f.read())\n            result.append({\"name\": parsed[\"name\"] or entry, \"description\": parsed[\"description\"]})\n    return result\n\n\ndef get_skill(name: str, skills_dir: str) -> dict | None:\n    \"\"\"Get a single skill with full content. Returns None if not found.\"\"\"\n    skill_path = os.path.join(skills_dir, name, \"SKILL.md\")\n    if not os.path.isfile(skill_path):\n        return None\n    with open(skill_path) as f:\n        return _parse_skill_md(f.read())\n\n\ndef create_skill(name: str, description: str, content: str, skills_dir: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Create a new skill. Raises SkillValidationError on invalid input or duplicate.\"\"\"\n    _validate_name(name)\n    skill_dir = os.path.join(skills_dir, name)\n    if os.path.isdir(skill_dir):\n        raise SkillValidationError(f\"Skill \\\"{name}\\\" already exists.\")\n    _write_skill_md(os.path.join(skill_dir, \"SKILL.md\"), name, description, content)\n\n\ndef update_skill(name: str, description: str, content: str, skills_dir: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Update an existing skill. Raises SkillValidationError if not found.\"\"\"\n    skill_path = os.path.join(skills_dir, name, \"SKILL.md\")\n    if not os.path.isfile(skill_path):\n        raise SkillValidationError(f\"Skill \\\"{name}\\\" not found.\")\n    _write_skill_md(skill_path, name, description, content)\n\n\ndef delete_skill(name: str, skills_dir: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Delete a skill directory. Raises SkillValidationError if not found.\"\"\"\n    skill_dir = os.path.join(skills_dir, name)\n    if not os.path.isdir(skill_dir):\n        raise SkillValidationError(f\"Skill \\\"{name}\\\" not found.\")\n    shutil.rmtree(skill_dir)\n```\n\n**Step 4: Run tests to verify they pass**\n\nRun: `cd backend && python -m pytest tests/test_skills.py -v`\nExpected: All 11 tests PASS\n\n**Step 5: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add backend/app/skills.py backend/tests/test_skills.py\ngit commit -m \"feat: add skill CRUD module with SKILL.md parsing\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### Task 2: Backend REST API for `backend/tests/test_skills_routes.py`:\n\n```python\nimport os\nimport pytest\nfrom unittest.mock import patch\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\nfrom app.main import
