```
     1→import type { SkillInfo } from '../types';
     2→
     3→export async function fetchSkills(): Promise<SkillInfo[]> {
     4→  const resp = await fetch('/api/skills');
     5→  if (!resp.ok) throw new Error('Failed to fetch skills');
     6→  return resp.json();
     7→}
     8→
     9→export async function fetchSkill(name: string): Promise<SkillInfo> {
    10→  const resp = await fetch(`/api/skills/${encodeURIComponent(name)}`);
    11→  if (!resp.ok) throw new Error(`Skill '${name}' not found`);
    12→  return resp.json();
    13→}
    14→
    15→export async function createSkill(skill: SkillInfo): Promise<SkillInfo> {
    16→  const resp = await fetch('/api/skills', {
    17→    method: 'POST',
    18→    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    19→    body: JSON.stringify(skill),
    20→  });
    21→  if (!resp.ok) {
    22→    const err = await resp.json();
    23→    throw new Error(err.error || 'Failed to create skill');
    24→  }
    25→  return resp.json();
    26→}
    27→
    28→export async function deleteSkill(name: string): Promise<void> {
    29→  const resp = await fetch(`/api/skills/${encodeURIComponent(name)}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
    30→  if (!resp.ok) throw new Error('Failed to delete skill');
    31→}
    32→

   112→                {skill.name}
   113→              </span>
   114→              {loadingDetail === skill.name && <span className="skills-panel__loading">...</span>}
   115→              <button
   116→                className="skills-panel__use-btn"
   117→                onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onUseSkill(skill.name); }}
   118→              >
   119→                {t('useSkill')}
   120→              </button>
   121→              <button
   122→                className="skills-panel__delete-btn"
   123→                onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); handleDelete(skill.name); }}
   124→                title={t('deleteSkill', { name: skill.name })}
   125→              >
   126→                &times;
   127→              </button>
   128→            </div>
   129→            <p className="skills-panel__desc skills-panel__desc--truncated">
   130→              {skill.description}
   131→            </p>
   132→          </li>
   133→        ))}
   134→      </ul>
   135→    </div>
   136→  );
   137→}
   138→
```

     1→import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
     2→import { useTranslation } from '../hooks/useTranslation';
     3→import { fetchSkills, fetchSkill, deleteSkill as apiDeleteSkill } from '../services/skillsService';
     4→import type { SkillInfo } from '../types';
     5→import './SkillsPanel.css';
     6→
     7→interface SkillsPanelProps {
     8→  onUseSkill: (skillName: string) => void;
     9→  onCreateClick: () => void;
    10→  refreshKey: number;
    11→}
    12→
    13→export function SkillsPanel({ onUseSkill, onCreateClick, refreshKey }: SkillsPanelProps) {
    14→  const { t } = useTranslation();
    15→  const [skills, setSkills] = useState<SkillInfo[]>([]);
    16→  const [selectedSkill, setSelectedSkill] = useState<SkillInfo | null>(null);
    17→  const [loadingDetail, setLoadingDetail] = useState<string | null>(null);
    18→
    19→  const loadSkills = useCallback(async () => {
    20→    try {
    21→      const data = await fetchSkills();
    22→      setSkills(data);
    23→    } catch {
    24→      // silently ignore
    25→    }
    26→  }, []);
    27→
    28→  useEffect(() => { loadSkills(); }, [loadSkills, refreshKey]);
    29→
    30→  useEffect(() => {
    31→    const handler = () => loadSkills();
    32→    window.addEventListener('skills-updated', handler);
    33→    return () => window.removeEventListener('skills-updated', handler);
    34→  }, [loadSkills]);
    35→
    36→  const handleSkillClick = async (name: string) => {
    37→    if (selectedSkill?.name === name) {
    38→      setSelectedSkill(null);
    39→      return;
    40→    }
    41→    setLoadingDetail(name);
    42→    try {
    43→      const detail = await fetchSkill(name);
    44→      setSelectedSkill(detail);
    45→    } catch {
    46→      // silently ignore
    47→    } finally {
    48→      setLoadingDetail(null);
    49→    }
    50→  };
    51→
    52→  const handleDelete = async (name: string) => {
    53→    if (!confirm(t('deleteSkillConfirm', { name }))) return;
    54→    try {
    55→      await apiDeleteSkill(name);
    56→      setSkills((prev) => prev.filter((s) => s.name !== name));
    57→      if (selectedSkill?.name === name) setSelectedSkill(null);
    58→    } catch {
    59→      // silently ignore
    60→    }
    61→  };
    62→
    63→  if (skills.length === 0) {
    64→    return (
    65→      <div className="skills-panel">
    66→        <div className="skills-panel__actions">
    67→          <button className="skills-panel__create-btn" onClick={onCreateClick} title={t('createSkill')}>
    68→            +
    69→          </button>
    70→        </div>
    71→        <p className="skills-panel__empty">{t('noSkills')}</p>
    72→      </div>
    73→    );
    74→  }
    75→
    76→  return (
    77→    <div className="skills-panel">
    78→      <div className="skills-panel__actions">
    79→        <button className="skills-panel__create-btn" onClick={onCreateClick} title={t('createSkill')}>
    80→          +
    81→        </button>
    82→      </div>
    83→      <ul className="skills-panel__list">
    84→        {skills.map((skill) => (
    85→          <li key={skill.name} className="skills-panel__item" onClick={() => handleSkillClick(skill.name)}>
    86→            <div className="skills-panel__item-header">
    87→              <span className="skills-panel__name" title={skill.name}>
    88→                {skill.name}
    89→              </span>
    90→              {loadingDetail === skill.name && <span className="skills-panel__loading">...</span>}
    91→              <button
    92→                className="skills-panel__use-btn"
    93→                onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onUseSkill(skill.name); }}
    94→              >
    95→                {t('useSkill')}
    96→              </button>
    97→              <button
    98→                className="skills-panel__delete-btn"
    99→                onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); handleDelete(skill.name); }}
   100→                title={t('deleteSkill', { name: skill.name })}
   101→              >
   102→                &times;
   103→              </button>
   104→            </div>
   105→            <p className="skills-panel__desc skills-panel__desc--truncated">
   106→              {skill.description}
   107→            </p>
   108→          </li>
   109→        ))}
   110→      </ul>
   111→
   112→      {selectedSkill && (
   113→        <div className="skill-detail-overlay" onClick={() => setSelectedSkill(null)}>
   114→          <div className="skill-detail-modal" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
   115→            <div className="skill-detail-modal__header">

     1→import ReactMarkdown from 'react-markdown';
     2→import { useTranslation } from '../hooks/useTranslation';
     3→import type { QueryResult } from '../types';
     4→import './ResultMarkdown.css';
     5→
     6→interface ResultMarkdownProps {
     7→  result: QueryResult | null;
     8→}
     9→
    10→export function ResultMarkdown({ result }: ResultMarkdownProps) {
    11→  const { t } = useTranslation();
    12→  if (!result) return null;
    13→
    14→  const text = result.rows
    15→    .map((row) => Object.values(row).join('\n'))
    16→    .join('\n');
    17→
    18→  const markdown = '```\n' + text + '\n```';
    19→
    20→  return (
    21→    <div className="result-markdown">
    22→      <div className="result-markdown__summary">
    23→        {t('rowCountInTime', { count: result.rowCount, time: result.executionTimeMs.toFixed(1) })}
    24→      </div>
    25→      <div className="result-markdown__content">
    26→        <ReactMarkdown>{markdown}</ReactMarkdown>
    27→      </div>
    28→    </div>
    29→  );
    30→}
    31→
```

     1→import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
     2→import ReactMarkdown from 'react-markdown';
     3→import remarkGfm from 'remark-gfm';
     4→import { useTranslation } from '../hooks/useTranslation';
     5→import { fetchSkills, fetchSkill, deleteSkill as apiDeleteSkill } from '../services/skillsService';
     6→import type { SkillInfo } from '../types';
     7→import './SkillsPanel.css';
     8→
     9→interface SkillsPanelProps {
    10→  onUseSkill: (skillName: string) => void;
    11→  onCreateClick: () => void;
    12→  refreshKey: number;
    13→}
    14→
    15→export function SkillsPanel({ onUseSkill, onCreateClick, refreshKey }: SkillsPanelProps) {
    16→  const { t } = useTranslation();
    17→  const [skills, setSkills] = useState<SkillInfo[]>([]);
    18→  const [selectedSkill, setSelectedSkill] = useState<SkillInfo | null>(null);
    19→  const [loadingDetail, setLoadingDetail] = useState<string | null>(null);
    20→
    21→  const loadSkills = useCallback(async () => {
    22→    try {
    23→      const data = await fetchSkills();
    24→      setSkills(data);
    25→    } catch {
    26→      // silently ignore
    27→    }
    28→  }, []);
    29→
    30→  useEffect(() => { loadSkills(); }, [loadSkills, refreshKey]);
    31→
    32→  useEffect(() => {
    33→    const handler = () => loadSkills();
    34→    window.addEventListener('skills-updated', handler);
    35→    return () => window.removeEventListener('skills-updated', handler);
    36→  }, [loadSkills]);
    37→
    38→  const handleSkillClick = async (name: string) => {
    39→    if (selectedSkill?.name === name) {
    40→      setSelectedSkill(null);
    41→      return;
    42→    }
    43→    setLoadingDetail(name);
    44→    try {
    45→      const detail = await fetchSkill(name);
    46→      setSelectedSkill(detail);
    47→    } catch {
    48→      // silently ignore
    49→    } finally {
    50→      setLoadingDetail(null);
    51→    }
    52→  };
    53→
    54→  const handleDelete = async (name: string) => {
    55→    if (!confirm(t('deleteSkillConfirm', { name }))) return;
    56→    try {
    57→      await apiDeleteSkill(name);
    58→      setSkills((prev) => prev.filter((s) => s.name !== name));
    59→      if (selectedSkill?.name === name) setSelectedSkill(null);
    60→    } catch {
    61→      // silently ignore
    62→    }
    63→  };
    64→
    65→  if (skills.length === 0) {
    66→    return (
    67→      <div className="skills-panel">
    68→        <div className="skills-panel__actions">
    69→          <button className="skills-panel__create-btn" onClick={onCreateClick} title={t('createSkill')}>
    70→            +
    71→          </button>
    72→        </div>
    73→        <p className="skills-panel__empty">{t('noSkills')}</p>
    74→      </div>
    75→    );
    76→  }
    77→
    78→  return (
    79→    <div className="skills-panel">
    80→      <div className="skills-panel__actions">
    81→        <button className="skills-panel__create-btn" onClick={onCreateClick} title={t('createSkill')}>
    82→          +
    83→        </button>
    84→      </div>
    85→      <ul className="skills-panel__list">
    86→        {skills.map((skill) => (
    87→          <li key={skill.name} className="skills-panel__item" onClick={() => handleSkillClick(skill.name)}>
    88→            <div className="skills-panel__item-header">
    89→              <span className="skills-panel__name" title={skill.name}>
    90→                {skill.name}
    91→              </span>
    92→              {loadingDetail === skill.name && <span className="skills-panel__loading">...</span>}
    93→              <button
    94→                className="skills-panel__use-btn"
    95→                onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onUseSkill(skill.name); }}
    96→              >
    97→                {t('useSkill')}
    98→              </button>
    99→              <button
   100→                className="skills-panel__delete-btn"
   101→                onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); handleDelete(skill.name); }}
   102→                title={t('deleteSkill', { name: skill.name })}
   103→              >
   104→                &times;
   105→              </button>
   106→            </div>
   107→            <p className="skills-panel__desc skills-panel__desc--truncated">
   108→              {skill.description}
   109→            </p>
   110→          </li>
   111→        ))}
   112→      </ul>
   113→
   114→      {selectedSkill && (
   115→        <div className="skill-detail-overlay" onClick={() => setSelectedSkill(null)}>
   116→          <div className="skill-detail-modal" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
   117→            <div className="skill-detail-modal__header">
   118→              <span className="skill-detail-modal__name">{selectedSkill.name}</span>
   119→              <button
   120→                className="skills-panel__use-btn"
   121→                onClick={() => onUseSkill(selectedSkill.name)}
   122→              >
   123→                {t('useSkill')}
   124→              </button>
   125→              <button className="skill-detail-modal__close" onClick={() => setSelectedSkill(null)}>
   126→                &times;
   127→              </button>
   128→            </div>
   129→            <p className="skill-detail-modal__desc">{selectedSkill.description}</p>
   130→            {selectedSkill.content && (
   131→              <pre className="skill-detail-modal__content">{selectedSkill.content}</pre>
   132→            )}
   133→          </div>
   134→        </div>
   135→      )}
   136→    </div>
   137→  );
   138→}
   139→

}\n.skill-detail-modal__content--preview h2 { font-size: 15px; }\n.skill-detail-modal__content--preview h3 { font-size: 13px; }\n\n.skill-detail-modal__content--preview p {\n  margin: 6px 0;\n}\n\n.skill-detail-modal__content--preview ul,\n.skill-detail-modal__content--preview ol {\n  margin: 6px 0;\n  padding-left: 20px;\n}\n\n.skill-detail-modal__content--preview code {\n  background: var(--color-bg-primary);\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border-light);\n  border-radius: 3px;\n  padding: 1px 4px;\n  font-size: 11px;\n  font-family: monospace;\n}\n\n.skill-detail-modal__content--preview pre {\n  background: var(--color-bg-primary);\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border-light);\n  border-radius: 6px;\n  padding: 8px;\n  margin: 6px 0;\n  overflow-x: auto;\n}\n\n.skill-detail-modal__content--preview pre code {\n  background: none;\n  border: none;\n  padding: 0;\n}\n\n.skill-detail-modal__content--preview table {\n  border-collapse: collapse;\n  width: 100%;\n  margin: 6px 0;\n  font-size: 12px;\n}\n\n.skill-detail-modal__content--preview th,\n.skill-detail-modal__content--preview td {\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border-light);\n  padding: 4px 8px;\n  text-align: left;\n}\n\n.skill-detail-modal__content--preview th {\n  background: var(--color-bg-primary);\n  font-weight: 600;\n}"

> AGENT

Done. The skill detail modal now has two tabs:

- **Preview** (default) — renders the markdown content with `react-markdown` + `remark-gfm`, supporting headings, lists, code blocks, tables, etc.
- **Source** — shows the raw markdown text in a monospace `<pre>` block

Changes:
- `SkillsPanel.tsx` — added `detailTab` state, tab buttons, and conditional `ReactMarkdown` / `<pre>` rendering
- `SkillsPanel.css` — added tab button styles and markdown preview typography (headings, code, tables, lists)
- `en.json` / `zh-TW.json` — added `skillPreview` and `skillSource` i18n keys

> AGENT

Done. Now the chat input shows `use this skill /analyze-data`.

Also updated the skill extraction in `AgentContext.tsx` to find `/skill-name` anywhere in the message (not just at the start).

Regarding your question about multiple skills in the chat input — currently the regex `\/([a-z0-9-]+)` only matches the **first** `/skill-name` it finds. If a user somehow has multiple slash commands like `use /skill-a and /skill-b`, only `/skill-a` would be extracted as the skill and `/skill-b` would remain in the message text.

Should I keep it as single-skill (first match wins), or do you want to support multiple skills in one message?

Done. The full chain now supports multiple skills in one message:

- **AgentContext.tsx** — extracts all `/skill-name` matches using `matchAll`, strips them from the message text

- **agent.py** — passes `skills` list to sidecar payload
- **sidecar types.ts** — `skills?: string[]`

### Backend Unit Tests (Python/pytest)

### Test Architecture

The E2E tests use a **YAML-driven approach**:
1. Test scenarios defined in YAML with declarative steps
2. Dynamic test generator reads YAML at runtime
3. Supports multiple action types: file uploads, message sending, UI clicks, waits, navigation
4. Two verification modes: structural (DOM assertions) and semantic (LLM-as-judge using Claude API)
5. Structured reporting with HTML output and optional on-failure screenshots

     1→import { Page, expect } from '@playwright/test';
     2→import * as path from 'path';
     3→import { Step } from './types';
     4→
     5→export async function executeStep(page: Page, step: Step, scenariosDir: string): Promise<void> {
     6→  switch (step.action) {
     7→    case 'upload_file':
     8→      return uploadFile(page, step.file, scenariosDir);
     9→    case 'send_message':
    10→      return sendMessage(page, step.input);
    11→    case 'wait_for_response':
    12→      return waitForResponse(page, step.timeout);
    13→    case 'click':
    14→      return clickElement(page, step.selector);
    15→    case 'navigate':
    16→      return navigate(page, step.path);
    17→    case 'verify':
    18→    case 'verify_llm':
    19→      return; // handled by verifiers, not actions
    20→  }
    21→}
    22→
    23→async function uploadFile(page: Page, filePath: string, scenariosDir: string): Promise<void> {
    24→  const absolutePath = path.resolve(scenariosDir, '..', filePath);
    25→  const fileInput = page.locator('.file-upload__input');
    26→  await fileInput.setInputFiles(absolutePath);
    27→  // Wait for upload to complete — sidebar table entry appears
    28→  await page.locator('.sidebar__table-name').first().waitFor({ state: 'visible', timeout: 15_000 });
    29→}
    30→
    31→async function sendMessage(page: Page, input: string): Promise<void> {
    32→  const textarea = page.locator('.chat-input__textarea');
    33→  await textarea.fill(input);
    34→  const sendBtn = page.locator('.chat-input__send');
    35→  await sendBtn.click();
    36→}
    37→
    38→async function waitForResponse(page: Page, timeout?: number): Promise<void> {
    39→  const effectiveTimeout = timeout || 120_000;
    40→  // Wait for the chat input textarea to become enabled again.
    41→  // While the agent is streaming, the textarea is disabled with
    42→  // placeholder "Waiting for response...". This is the most reliable
    43→  // signal that the full response (including subagent work) is complete.
    44→  const textarea = page.locator('.chat-input__textarea');
    45→  await expect(textarea).toBeEnabled({ timeout: effectiveTimeout });
    46→}
    47→
    48→async function clickElement(page: Page, selector: string): Promise<void> {
    49→  await page.locator(selector).click();
    50→}
    51→
    52→async function navigate(page: Page, urlPath: string): Promise<void> {
    53→  await page.goto(urlPath);
    54→}
    55→

     1→import { Page, expect } from '@playwright/test';
     2→import { VerifyStep } from './types';
     3→
     4→export async function runStructuralVerify(page: Page, step: VerifyStep): Promise<void> {
     5→  const expected = step.expected;
     6→  // Get the last assistant message bubble for text checks
     7→  const lastAssistant = page.locator('.message-bubble--assistant').last();
     8→
     9→  if (expected.contains) {
    10→    const text = await lastAssistant.innerText();
    11→    for (const keyword of expected.contains) {
    12→      expect(text.toLowerCase()).toContain(keyword.toLowerCase());
    13→    }
    14→  }
    15→
    16→  if (expected.not_contains) {
    17→    const text = await lastAssistant.innerText();
    18→    for (const keyword of expected.not_contains) {
    19→      expect(text.toLowerCase()).not.toContain(keyword.toLowerCase());
    20→    }
    21→  }
    22→
    23→  if (expected.has_chart === true) {
    24→    await expect(lastAssistant.locator('.js-plotly-plot')).toBeVisible();
    25→  }
    26→  if (expected.has_chart === false) {
    27→    await expect(lastAssistant.locator('.js-plotly-plot')).not.toBeVisible();
    28→  }
    29→
    30→  if (expected.has_table === true) {
    31→    await expect(lastAssistant.locator('.results-table__table')).toBeVisible();
    32→  }
    33→  if (expected.has_table === false) {
    34→    await expect(lastAssistant.locator('.results-table__table')).not.toBeVisible();
    35→  }
    36→
    37→  if (expected.table_row_count_min !== undefined) {
    38→    const rows = lastAssistant.locator('.results-table__table tbody tr');
    39→    const count = await rows.count();
    40→    expect(count).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(expected.table_row_count_min);
    41→  }
    42→
    43→  if (expected.element_exists) {
    44→    await expect(page.locator(expected.element_exists)).toBeVisible();
    45→  }
    46→
    47→  if (expected.element_not_exists) {
    48→    await expect(page.locator(expected.element_not_exists)).not.toBeVisible();
    49→  }
    50→
    51→  if (expected.css_property) {
    52→    const el = page.locator(expected.css_property.selector);
    53→    const value = await el.evaluate(
    54→      (node, prop) => getComputedStyle(node).getPropertyValue(prop),
    55→      expected.css_property.property
    56→    );
    57→    expect(value).toMatch(new RegExp(expected.css_property.matches));
    58→  }
    59→}
    60→

> AGENT

Now I have the full picture. Here's the answer:

**Yes, clearing chat history does make the agent forget** — but through two mechanisms:

1. **Frontend** (`AgentContext.tsx:611-619`): `clearMessages` does three things:
   - `setMessages([])` — clears the UI message list
   - `sessionIdRef.current = null` — **resets the session ID**
   - `pendingHistoryRef.current = null` — clears any pending history

2. **Sidecar resume** (`server.ts:283`): The sidecar uses `session_id` to **resume** the Claude Agent SDK conversation. When `session_id` is `null` (after clear), the next message starts a brand new SDK session with no prior context.

3. **Conversation history** (`agentService.ts:120`): The frontend also sends `conversation_history` as a fallback. After clear, `history` is `[]`, so no prior messages are sent.

So the agent truly forgets — it gets no `session_id` to resume from and no `conversation_history` to reference. The sidecar starts a fresh SDK session.

docs/survey-agent-sdk-plugins.md:7:The plugin system solves the problem of **reusable, shareable, and composable agent extensions**. Rather than configuring each extension point individually (skills in `.claude/skills/`, hooks in settings, MCP servers in `.mcp.json`), plugins bundle all of these into a single directory with a manifest file. Plugins can be distributed via marketplaces, installed with a single command, version-controlled, and shared across projects and teams.

docs/plans/2026-03-01-sidecar-skill-setup-design.md:8:The sidecar (Claude Agent SDK in Docker) has no skills configured. Adding a data analysis workflow skill will guide the agent through structured analysis steps, improving response quality for business analyst users.
docs/plans/2026-03-01-sidecar-skill-setup-design.md:14:Skills are defined as `sidecar/.claude/skills/<name>/SKILL.md` files and copied into the Docker image at build time. The SDK discovers them via `settingSources: ["project"]`.
docs/plans/2026-03-01-sidecar-skill-setup-design.md:16:**Why not runtime/dynamic skills?** The sidecar container runs `read_only=True` with tmpfs at `/home/appuser`. Writing to `/app/.claude/skills/` is not possible at runtime. Dynamic skill creation is a future enhancement requiring a persistent Docker volume.

docs/plans/2026-03-01-sidecar-skill-setup.md:5:**Goal:** Add a data analysis workflow skill to the sidecar so the Claude Agent SDK discovers and uses it automatically, with an allowlist hook to block built-in skills.
docs/plans/2026-03-01-sidecar-skill-setup.md:7:**Architecture:** Skill files live in `sidecar/.claude/skills/`, are COPY'd into the Docker image at `/app/.claude/skills/`, and the SDK loads them via `settingSources: ["project"]` with `"Skill"` in `allowedTools`. A `PreToolUse` hook dynamically discovers allowed skills from the `.claude/skills/` directory and blocks any built-in skills not in the allowlist.

     1→import os
     2→import re
     3→import shutil
     4→
     5→
     6→class SkillValidationError(Exception):
     7→    pass
     8→
     9→
    10→_NAME_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^[a-z0-9-]+$")
    11→
    12→
    13→def _validate_name(name: str) -> None:
    14→    if not name or len(name) > 64 or not _NAME_PATTERN.match(name):
    15→        raise SkillValidationError(
    16→            "Skill name must be 1-64 characters, lowercase alphanumeric with hyphens."
    17→        )
    18→
    19→
    20→def _parse_skill_md(text: str) -> dict:
    21→    """Parse a SKILL.md file into {name, description, content}."""
    22→    if not text.startswith("---"):
    23→        return {"name": "", "description": "", "content": text}
    24→    try:
    25→        end = text.index("---", 3)
    26→    except ValueError:
    27→        return {"name": "", "description": "", "content": text}
    28→    frontmatter = text[3:end].strip()
    29→    body = text[end + 3:].strip()
    30→    meta: dict[str, str] = {}
    31→    for line in frontmatter.splitlines():
    32→        if ":" in line:
    33→            key, _, val = line.partition(":")
    34→            meta[key.strip()] = val.strip()
    35→    return {"name": meta.get("name", ""), "description": meta.get("description", ""), "content": body}
    36→
    37→
    38→def _write_skill_md(path: str, name: str, description: str, content: str) -> None:
    39→    """Write a SKILL.md file with YAML frontmatter."""
    40→    text = f"---\nname: {name}\ndescription: {description}\n---\n\n{content}\n"
    41→    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path), exist_ok=True)
    42→    with open(path, "w") as f:
    43→        f.write(text)
    44→
    45→
    46→def list_skills(skills_dir: str) -> list[dict]:
    47→    """List all skills (name + description only)."""
    48→    if not os.path.isdir(skills_dir):
    49→        return []
    50→    result = []
    51→    for entry in sorted(os.listdir(skills_dir)):
    52→        skill_path = os.path.join(skills_dir, entry, "SKILL.md")
    53→        if os.path.isdir(os.path.join(skills_dir, entry)) and os.path.isfile(skill_path):
    54→            with open(skill_path) as f:
    55→                parsed = _parse_skill_md(f.read())
    56→            result.append({"name": parsed["name"] or entry, "description": parsed["description"]})
    57→    return result
    58→
    59→
    60→def get_skill(name: str, skills_dir: str) -> dict | None:
    61→    """Get a single skill with full content. Returns None if not found."""
    62→    skill_path = os.path.join(skills_dir, name, "SKILL.md")
    63→    if not os.path.isfile(skill_path):
    64→        return None
    65→    with open(skill_path) as f:
    66→        return _parse_skill_md(f.read())
    67→
    68→
    69→def create_skill(name: str, description: str, content: str, skills_dir: str) -> None:
    70→    """Create a new skill. Raises SkillValidationError on invalid input or duplicate."""
    71→    _validate_name(name)
    72→    skill_dir = os.path.join(skills_dir, name)
    73→    if os.path.isdir(skill_dir):
    74→        raise SkillValidationError(f"Skill \"{name}\" already exists.")
    75→    _write_skill_md(os.path.join(skill_dir, "SKILL.md"), name, description, content)
    76→
    77→
    78→def update_skill(name: str, description: str, content: str, skills_dir: str) -> None:
    79→    """Update an existing skill. Raises SkillValidationError if not found."""
    80→    skill_path = os.path.join(skills_dir, name, "SKILL.md")
    81→    if not os.path.isfile(skill_path):
    82→        raise SkillValidationError(f"Skill \"{name}\" not found.")
    83→    _write_skill_md(skill_path, name, description, content)
    84→
    85→
    86→def delete_skill(name: str, skills_dir: str) -> None:
    87→    """Delete a skill directory. Raises SkillValidationError if not found."""
    88→    skill_dir = os.path.join(skills_dir, name)
    89→    if not os.path.isdir(skill_dir):
    90→        raise SkillValidationError(f"Skill \"{name}\" not found.")
    91→    shutil.rmtree(skill_dir)
    92→
```

     1→import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';

     4→import { useTranslation } from '../hooks/useTranslation';
     5→import { fetchSkills, fetchSkill, deleteSkill as apiDeleteSkill } from '../services/skillsService';
     6→import type { SkillInfo } from '../types';
     7→import './SkillsPanel.css';
     8→
     9→interface SkillsPanelProps {
    10→  onUseSkill: (skillName: string) => void;
    11→  onCreateClick: () => void;
    12→  refreshKey: number;
    13→}
    14→
    15→export function SkillsPanel({ onUseSkill, onCreateClick, refreshKey }: SkillsPanelProps) {
    16→  const { t } = useTranslation();
    17→  const [skills, setSkills] = useState<SkillInfo[]>([]);
    18→  const [selectedSkill, setSelectedSkill] = useState<SkillInfo | null>(null);
    19→  const [loadingDetail, setLoadingDetail] = useState<string | null>(null);
    20→  const [detailTab, setDetailTab] = useState<'preview' | 'source'>('preview');
    21→
    22→  const loadSkills = useCallback(async () => {
    23→    try {
    24→      const data = await fetchSkills();
    25→      setSkills(data);
    26→    } catch {
    27→      // silently ignore
    28→    }
    29→  }, []);
    30→
    31→  useEffect(() => { loadSkills(); }, [loadSkills, refreshKey]);
    32→
    33→  useEffect(() => {
    34→    const handler = () => loadSkills();
    35→    window.addEventListener('skills-updated', handler);
    36→    return () => window.removeEventListener('skills-updated', handler);
    37→  }, [loadSkills]);
    38→
    39→  const handleSkillClick = async (name: string) => {
    40→    if (selectedSkill?.name === name) {
    41→      setSelectedSkill(null);
    42→      return;
    43→    }
    44→    setLoadingDetail(name);
    45→    setDetailTab('preview');
    46→    try {
    47→      const detail = await fetchSkill(name);
    48→      setSelectedSkill(detail);
    49→    } catch {
    50→      // silently ignore
    51→    } finally {
    52→      setLoadingDetail(null);
    53→    }
    54→  };
    55→
    56→  const handleDelete = async (name: string) => {
    57→    if (!confirm(t('deleteSkillConfirm', { name }))) return;
    58→    try {
    59→      await apiDeleteSkill(name);
    60→      setSkills((prev) => prev.filter((s) => s.name !== name));
    61→      if (selectedSkill?.name === name) setSelectedSkill(null);
    62→    } catch {
    63→      // silently ignore
    64→    }
    65→  };
    66→
    67→  if (skills.length === 0) {
    68→    return (
    69→      <div className="skills-panel">
    70→        <div className="skills-panel__actions">
    71→          <button className="skills-panel__create-btn" onClick={onCreateClick} title={t('createSkill')}>
    72→            +
    73→          </button>
    74→        </div>
    75→        <p className="skills-panel__empty">{t('noSkills')}</p>
    76→      </div>
    77→    );
    78→  }
    79→
    80→  return (
    81→    <div className="skills-panel">
    82→      <div className="skills-panel__actions">
    83→        <button className="skills-panel__create-btn" onClick={onCreateClick} title={t('createSkill')}>
    84→          +
    85→        </button>
    86→      </div>
    87→      <ul className="skills-panel__list">
    88→        {skills.map((skill) => (
    89→          <li key={skill.name} className="skills-panel__item" onClick={() => handleSkillClick(skill.name)}>
    90→            <div className="skills-panel__item-header">
    91→              <span className="skills-panel__name" title={skill.name}>
    92→                {skill.name}
    93→              </span>
    94→              {loadingDetail === skill.name && <span className="skills-panel__loading">...</span>}
    95→              <button
    96→                className="skills-panel__use-btn"
    97→                onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onUseSkill(skill.name); }}
    98→              >
    99→                {t('useSkill')}
   100→              </button>
   101→              <button
   102→                className="skills-panel__delete-btn"
   103→                onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); handleDelete(skill.name); }}
   104→                title={t('deleteSkill', { name: skill.name })}
   105→              >

Perfect! Now I have a comprehensive understanding of the skill data flow. Let me create a detailed report.

## Comprehensive Skill Data Flow Analysis

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here's the complete data flow for skills:

### 1. **SKILL.md Frontmatter Parsing** (`/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/sidecar-skill/backend/app/skills.py`)

The `_parse_skill_md()` function (lines 20-35) parses SKILL.md files with YAML frontmatter:

```python
def _parse_skill_md(text: str) -> dict:
    """Parse a SKILL.md file into {name, description, content}."""
    # Currently parsed fields:
    # - name: Skill identifier (required)
    # - description: Short description for UI display (required)
    # - content: The full skill definition/instructions (from body)
```

**Currently extracted fields:**
- `name` - Skill identifier from frontmatter
- `description` - Skill description from frontmatter
- `content` - Full body content after frontmatter separator

**Note:** There is **no parsing of a disabled/enabled field** currently. The function extracts only `name` and `description` from the frontmatter, defaulting to empty strings if missing.

### 2. **Backend API Service** (`/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/sidecar-skill/backend/app/routes/skills.py`)

Routes are registered at `/api/skills` prefix:

- **`GET /api/skills`** → `api_list_skills()` calls `list_skills(SKILLS_DIR)`
  - Returns: `list[dict]` with `{name, description}` for each skill
  - Iterates through `skills/` directory, reads `SKILL.md`, extracts name/description only

- **`GET /api/skills/{name}`** → `api_get_skill(name)`
  - Returns: Full parsed skill `{name, description, content}`
  - Returns 404 if skill not found

- **`POST /api/skills`** → Creates new skill
- **`PUT /api/skills/{name}`** → Updates existing skill
- **`DELETE /api/skills/{name}`** → Deletes skill

**SKILLS_DIR** is configured via environment variable, defaulting to `../../../skills` relative to the backend directory.

### 3. **Frontend Service** (`/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/sidecar-skill/frontend/src/services/skillsService.ts`)

Simple fetch wrappers that call the backend API:

```typescript
export async function fetchSkills(): Promise<SkillInfo[]>
// GET /api/skills → Returns SkillInfo[]

export async function fetchSkill(name: string): Promise<SkillInfo>
// GET /api/skills/{name} → Returns SkillInfo with content
```

No filtering or transformation happens here—raw backend responses are returned.

### 4. **Autocomplete Menu** (`/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/sidecar-skill/frontend/src/components/SlashCommandMenu.tsx`)

The `SlashCommandMenu` component filters skills client-side:

```typescript
const filtered = skills.filter((s) =>
  s.name.includes(filter.toLowerCase())
);
```

**Filtering logic:**
- Case-insensitive substring match on skill `name` only
- No filtering based on description or any disabled/enabled status
- All skills returned from the API are shown in the autocomplete

The skill is selected via `filtered[selectedIndex].name`, and the user message becomes `/{skillName}`.

### 5. **Sidecar Skill Discovery** (`/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/sidecar-skill/sidecar/src/server.ts`, lines 26-33)

```typescript
function discoverSkills(): Set<string> {
  if (!existsSync(SKILLS_DIR)) return new Set();
  return new Set(
    readdirSync(SKILLS_DIR, { withFileTypes: true })
      .filter((d) => d.isDirectory() && existsSync(join(SKILLS_DIR, d.name, "SKILL.md")))
      .map((d) => d.name)
  );
}
```

**Current behavior:**
- Returns all skill folder names that contain a `SKILL.md` file
- **Does NOT parse SKILL.md** to check for disabled status
- Only criterion: directory exists with `SKILL.md` file

**Used for:**
- Skill allowlist enforcement via `skillAllowlistHook` (lines 38-57)
- System prompt skill restriction messaging (lines 237-241)

### 6. **SkillInfo Type** (`/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/sidecar-skill/frontend/src/types.ts`, lines 90-94)

```typescript
export interface SkillInfo {
  name: string;
  description: string;
  content?: string;  // Optional, populated by GET /api/skills/{name}
}
```

**Note:** No `disabled` or `enabled` field exists. Type is minimal—only what's parsed from frontmatter.

### 7. **Sample Skill: analyze-data** (`/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/sidecar-skill/skills/analyze-data/SKILL.md`)

```markdown
---
name: analyze-data
description: Guides structured data analysis workflows using DuckDB...
---
# Data Analysis Workflow
[full instructions...]
```

**Frontmatter fields:**
- `name: analyze-data`
- `description: [narrative description]`
- No other fields currently defined

---

## Key Insights & Gaps

1. **No disabled field support yet** — The parsing infrastructure only extracts `name` and `description`. A `disabled: true` field would be ignored.

2. **Sidecar doesn't parse frontmatter** — `discoverSkills()` only checks for directory + `SKILL.md` existence, not frontmatter content. Any filtering would require parsing.

3. **Frontend filtering is name-only** — SlashCommandMenu filters on skill name substring match. No description or status filtering.

4. **No explicit allowlist in .claude/skills/** — The system checks the `skills/` directory directly. There's no separate `.claude/skills/` allowlist file mentioned in the current implementation.

5. **Skill restriction happens at sidecar level** — The PreToolUse hook in `server.ts` (lines 38-57) blocks skills not in `discoverSkills()` result, protecting against both disabled skills and built-in skill collisions if filtering were added.
